Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

11

Venus in the Air Signs

When Venus is in any of the Air signs, verbal communication and intellectual stimulation are necessities for a close relationship or deep friendship to flourish. A great deal of personal emotion and affection is routed through the thinking part of the mind and has to acquire the approval of the intellectual part of the personality before the deeper dimensions of the relationship can really begin to flow. In other words, a “meeting of minds” is the key to this person’s feeling satisfied with any close relationship, romantic or otherwise. They may indeed like to flirt with a wide variety of people in the romantic sphere, but a sense of true companionship will be required for a long-lasting, satisfying relationship. More than any others, the Venus signs in the Air element are the most
personal
 and the most seriously interested in other people’s thoughts, experiences, and point of view. (The reader should review the Venus in the Air signs section of
Chapter 8
 for my previous discussion of Venus in this intensely intellectual, social element, including my brief
comparisons
 of each of these three Venus signs.)

Other keys to understanding Venus in the Air signs are as follows:

To feel comfortable in a relationship, the person has to feel (at least unconsciously) that he or she has “figured out” how this personal exchange fits into their world of concepts and ideas. There is a powerful need to
categorize
 the relationship—if only so they can know they are breaking the rules, like Aquarius Venus so often does. The possible partner will see them constantly observing and thinking
along these lines, before they can get actively engaged or committed to putting time and energy into the relationship. It seems a great number of those with Venus in the Air signs keep a journal or diary or in some other form write out their experiences in an attempt to sort out their feelings.

All those with Venus in Air signs are light, playful, and instinctively flirtatious. They all need a sense of freedom; however, flirtation does not in any way mean that emotional or physical activity will necessarily be on the menu.

Because Gemini and Aquarius are so experimental, those with Venus in these signs often try anything in the realm of relationships, in a rather disinterested, curious way; for they are not as personal as they seem. Venus in Libra, however, is more cautious and traditional; they are so personal, serious, and aesthetically oriented that their approach is far more conservative.

Venus in Gemini

If Venus is located in the changeable, lively, endlessly curious sign of Gemini, there is almost invariably a broad range of interests and remarkable social skills. A versatility with words is evident—whether in speech and sales skills, publishing or editing endeavors, or linguistic gifts. I’ve so often observed such considerable aesthetic cleverness in these folks that I finally settled upon the term
intelligent taste
 to describe their discriminating appreciation for the details of many forms of art and creativity. They can get along with a huge variety of people because they are so diplomatic, tactful, and authentically curious. In fact, they need varied friendships and good intellectual rapport with friends and partners. However, they can be so easily bored and fickle in their interests that many relationships become burdensome rather rapidly. The duality of this sign of the twins reveals
perhaps the biggest cause of problems in their lives—that everything is relative and thus it is hard to be satisfied with any relationship. In other words, they can lack depth of feeling, no matter how charming or animated they seem when a relationship is new. At worst, they can appear two-faced, saying one thing while thinking another. One should not judge them too harshly, however, since they do not seem to control both sides of their own mind, and they are often confused about feelings and affections.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 G
EMINI

After many years of working with and observing those with Venus in Gemini, I have distilled their core psychological qualities as follows:

Expresses affections verbally, cleverly, lightly, and playfully.

A need for variety and constant new stimuli can inhibit chances for lasting relationships.

Mental rapport is highly valued; attracted to quick wit; needs to talk immediately about one’s thoughts and perceptions to feel close to another.

In both sexes, Venus in Gemini can be so broadly curious and disembodied that their natural open-mindedness can manifest as chronic lack of clarity about intimate relationships. They are sometimes so mental in their approach to expressing affection and
eros
 that they can feel constantly confused about their desires, love needs, and sexual identity. Their energy, as well as their interest in relationships, is fitful, coming in bursts of activity followed by aimless inactivity (except for thinking). They can keep more than one significant relationship going at a time, and—as one interviewee stated—“I really do need to relate to lots of people, and the freedom to do this and to express myself has to be accepted for any close relationship to be long term.” To the surprise of more jealous types of people, those with Gemini Venus
often continue friendly relations past the point where a relationship ceases to be an intense “love” partnership. These folks desperately need grounding and structure to stabilize their emotional fluctuations; otherwise they are all over the place. In addition, especially when they are in their discovery and experimental phase, they tend to spontaneously share intimate details about their lives with every friendly stranger and colleague (something they can later regret), which can often irritate people they are getting involved with.

The women with Venus in Gemini are usually not particularly domestic
unless
 they also have a strong emphasis on Cancer in their birth charts. They love flirting, especially verbal games, including by telephone and e-mail. But they usually convey more interest in sexuality than they really feel or can express easily on the physical level. These ladies have a quite refined nature, which is often reflected in their beautiful hands. One woman with this position emphasized how important hand contact and lots of interesting talk were in building up her sexual interest—the hand contact being particularly erotic for a very intellectual woman with limited experience in sexual relationships.

Men who have a Gemini Venus love logic and analysis, as well as verbal or intellectual games, and they are usually quite pleased with their own intelligence. They are attracted to women who are unpredictable, curious, versatile, and who thus keep them guessing—even if it drives them crazy emotionally. The woman’s reasonableness, intellectual interests, and conversational ability are far more important to them than physical appeal alone.

Among the most perceptive descriptions of how Venus in Gemini is expressed are the following excerpts from interviews and questionnaire responses:

A woman with Gemini Venus wrote, “They like to impress you with their mind and cleverness, as well as with their social connections.
Someone being interested in her details and point of view seems like foreplay through the verbal banter” (AQ).

Another woman with Venus in Gemini said: “Daily life’s overwhelming stimuli can rattle my nervous system! Sex calms me down and helps me get out of my head and connect with my body and emotions” (AI).

Another woman said in an interview, “I value the intellectual part of me. I feel really good about my ideas. If someone can’t
appreciate
 that part of me, I can’t have a relationship with them” (AI).

Numerous interviewees with Venus in Gemini said that they like to spend lots of time talking about their feelings, but also that their constant thinking kept them from being in touch with their emotions. A number of questionnaires revealed that a common emotional outlet for many women with Venus in Gemini is keeping a journal to help sort out their feelings by writing them down, since they have a mental approach to feelings and relationships. (AI and AQ)

A woman who had known several men with Venus in Gemini observed that they had “shallow and self-serving ideas” and therefore had “a gift for rationalizing infidelity on their part.” Other interviewees revealed how Venus in Gemini is “afraid of any deep commitment.” (AQ and AI)

Venus in Libra

When Venus is in its own sign of Libra (of which it has been called the “ruling planet” for more than two millennia), there is an extra emphasis on appreciation of beauty, one-to-one relationship, and interpersonal harmony in the individual’s life. Expressions or examples of beauty
feed
 them, and they prefer to express a sweet, gentle disposition
(unless something else in the birth chart inhibits it). Sharing, fairness, and an obsession with seeing the other person’s point of view dominate the person’s psychological nature and motivations. They are eminently companionable and capable of meeting other people on their own level, perhaps more than any other sign. Although not particularly emotional, they are invariably romantic and often sentimental. They like to be “in love” and will often compromise excessively or give others the benefit of the doubt to their own detriment. These are excellent qualities for many life challenges and types of work, but sometimes this proclivity to diminish the value of one’s own viewpoint can undermine one’s strong desire to build enduring egalitarian relationships. Honesty and sincerity are core values, and there is perhaps nothing that offends them so much as injustice—particularly being unjustly accused of any impropriety or dishonorable action. Even an intimation of that can irreparably destroy a long-term friendship or deep relationship. They in fact have a horrible reaction to personal rejection, and more than any other sign, Libra takes everything
personally
.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 L
IBRA

The most accurate way of describing the psychological tendencies of Venus in Libra is embodied in the following:

Expresses affection sincerely, considerately, charmingly, and harmoniously.

A deep need for peace, tranquility, and harmony can lead to avoidance of unpleasant emotional exchanges and thus limit the scope of intimacy.

Needs to develop relationships based on equal sharing and cooperation in order to give of one’s emotions; appreciates symmetry and traditional beauty.

Both men and women who have Venus in Libra prefer a harmonious, respectful approach to close relationships. They can both be rather cold—not particularly passionate (although the other planetary positions in a chart, especially Mars, can alter this), and yet personally sympathetic. Being extraordinarily
refined, considerate
, and
nice
 count for everything with these folks. Both sexes tend to be good-looking, with balanced features, and they exemplify pleasing taste through their clothes and demeanor. Libra is a quite conservative sign in most areas of life and values, something not emphasized in most books on astrology. The men as well as the women with this placement are very idealistic in love and marriage, often to the point of being naïve. They meet the other person on an even level, needing at least as much interpersonal input as they themselves are able to provide stimulating output. Both sexes are often offended by any gross expressions, of sex or anything else. (Libra is
the
 sign of partnership and marriage, traditionally, and a stable, cooperative partnership is particularly important for these people.) Generally, those with Libra Venus are very faithful, since they have an especially
personal
 form of commitment.

Women with a Libra Venus invariably get attention for their natural beauty and grace, which sometimes manifest as classic features and/or body shape. They tend to be feminine and yet are surprisingly aloof—very often intelligent but cool and distant—and somewhat conventional in attitudes toward love and sex. Not very sensual and actually quite passive in intimate exchanges, they can express very personal feelings when they feel secure. A craving for tranquility and harmony in daily life is essential for them, and anyone who can help provide that atmosphere will be extraordinarily valued. Very aesthetically attuned, Venus in Libra exhibits a personality marked by a strong mental/artistic idealism. Interviews with these folks often evoked such expressions as: “Music is my passion!” These women are quite exclusive—not promiscuous at all—for a truly personal relationship is
needed for them to flower. They are very high-strung (although they seek to hide it) and do not easily relax into sexual intimacy, especially when under stress or with an unfamiliar person. And yet, they need to
relax
 to enjoy sex since they prefer to be
made love to
 rather than taking an active role.

If Venus is in Libra in a man’s chart, he insists on harmony in the relationship (although he may like energetic debate or discussion). He is naturally attracted to a phenomenon that is becoming rare in the Western world: a “lady”—someone who is dignified, graceful, polite, and tolerant. Hence, in recent times, this man often pairs off with women from foreign cultures or non-Western backgrounds so that he can satisfy his aesthetic needs. Venus in Libra men do not care for impulsiveness or exaggerated emotional displays, especially in their closest partnership. They like having an exclusive, special relationship based on sincerity, reliability, and trust.

The following quotations from various writings, interviews, and questionnaires are helpful in filling out the full spectrum of Libra Venus:

The most successful basketball coach in history, John Wooden, whose Libra influence helped him to understand this team sport based on interpersonal timing and chemistry, said in a 1989 interview, “Love and balance are the only things you need.” He was talking about life as well as sports.
Love
 and
balance
 have been the keynote concepts of Libra for more than 2,000 years.

A man in his thirties wrote, “Venus in Libra often has a classic body and good looks, but is very vain. Incurably romantic. They have ideals
about
 relationships that put a distance from
actual
 relationships” (AQ).

These people are extraordinarily visual, aesthetically and sensually. Not only are the women put off by demonstrations of grossness and pleasantly intrigued by stylish and tasteful appearance, but the
men are very susceptible to sensual/erotic images as well. One man with Venus in Libra (and Taurus rising, the other Venus sign!) was almost addicted to observing women shopping in the lingerie section of a major store. So attracted to image and the outer form of the partner are these folks that many prefer to make love with the light on, which might be inhibiting to those more interested in sinking into deeper feelings. (AI)

Grant Lewi notes, “The difficulties of life don’t destroy your faith in the beauty of love, and since this is the sort of faith that vindicates itself, you are likely to find more genuine happiness in life than most. You are easily hurt, but you aren’t touchy about it; you can’t hold a grudge or even condemn those who hurt you. You have a knack for thinking the best of people who repay you by thinking the best of you. The directness of your response to social relations, the genuineness of your affections, which you express with a combination of sincerity and impersonality—these endear you to many, make you a sought-after companion and guest and a charming host or hostess” (AFM).

A woman in her forties said about Venus in Libra: “I have found these people to be very sympathetic, understanding and tolerant; besides that, they make themselves easy to get along with … and seem to have an inner sense of harmony and value aesthetic matters highly” (AQ).

Venus in Aquarius

When Venus is placed in the detached, experimental, unpredictable sign of Aquarius, the individual has a wide variety of stimulating, often highly intelligent or creative friends. In fact, the freedom to associate with as many types of people, and in as many ways, as the person desires is something these folks insist upon. And no partnership
that inhibits that freedom will last long. Their attitude toward love is thus also very open, liberal, and unconventional. But although they so embrace freedom, paradoxically they like feeling
in control
 in relationships, and they can display an extreme, contrary streak now and then that carries an emotional charge they would like to feel they have transcended. They often don’t even realize that their individualistic (and even rebellious or antisocial) statements, beliefs, and behavior may offend others or emotionally repel them. Folks with Venus in this sign can’t stand jealousy, and exercising their intellectual freedom and excitedly communicating what they are learning or observing are paramount in their lives. They feel instinctively that the best way to show affection and appreciation to another is to give that person space and personal freedom. Both men and women who have Venus in Aquarius like to impress others with how modern and eclectic they are. They can even have positively weird tastes, a good example being the singer Elton John, whose onstage costumes are totally outrageous and sometimes hysterically funny.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 A
QUARIUS

The most precise way I can describe the psychological qualities and tendencies of Venus in Aquarius, a strong factor in many people I have known well, is as follows:

Expresses affection freely, unconventionally, flirtatiously, often humorously, and experimentally.

Values individualistic freedom and tends toward extremism and rebelliousness; detached, impersonal attitude can interfere with close relationships.

Enjoys exchange of theories and ideas; needs active socializing with many people in order to give fully of one’s emotional nature.

Men and women born with Venus in Aquarius feel rather uncomfortable with emotional intensity. A far greater pleasure for them is a cool, detached, “objective” exchange of ideas on the mental level. And, the more unusual or “different” from the social norm another person is, the more fascinating they find him or her. They revel in demonstrating how unpossessive, broad-minded, and understanding they are about human nature and human behavior. This is all well and good for ordinary friendships, but the amazingly detached approach they demonstrate puts definite obstacles in the road to one-to-one intimacy. They can be easily attracted to someone not only through the mind but also through the eye, but they are not attuned to the subtle and profound sense of touch (unless Earth or Water factors are also strong in the birth chart). In short, these folks are very
observant
 in love and sex—but detached as they observe. Hence, they are often awkward when it comes down to actual participation in sex or other physical contact—it is as if they were stuck in their heads. It sometimes seems as if they sense the huge gap between their mental life and the demands of the physical life, which they also persist in trying to approach through the mind.

Women with an Aquarius Venus are often quite charming, stimulating, and even charismatic in some cases. They really like flirtation, humor, and verbal exchange and play. They are easily attracted to people they can joke with or who are especially brilliant or original. Because of their tremendous independence and powerful insistence upon social freedom, they rather baffle most men. But the most difficult aspect of their personality for most men to handle is their remarkable
detachment
, which so often conveys a lack of caring and emotional commitment. Julia Parker’s comments on this placement particularly describe Venus in Aquarius women: “… a glamorous placement for Venus. Here is someone who has in their own individual way a kind of star quality and dynamic powers of attraction. But there is a
distinct tendency to distance themselves from partners, so that deep and meaningful emotional relationships are not always fully attained.… Venus in Aquarius is not high on affection and warmth.… however there is no lack of kindness” (AHB).

When a man has Venus in Aquarius in his birth chart, he is particularly attracted to a woman who is intelligent, unconventional, and experimental. The more intellectually sophisticated she is, or at least the more unusual her interests and insights, the more magnetic she appears to him. In his own emotions and attitudes toward relationships, he is quite frustrating to most people, unless they have a lot of Aquarius attunement also. One man who has both Sun and Venus in Aquarius, who after six years of marriage was asked by his wife to sit down and talk about the problems they were having, replied, “I don’t want to talk about that—it’s personal.”

The following quotes are among the most perceptive insights from many writings, interviews, and questionnaire replies:

One man in his thirties wrote about men with this placement: “Likes women from different ethnic backgrounds and has sexual fantasies of unusual types—especially intrigued if the woman is ‘different’ ” (AQ).

A woman in her fifties who has three male family members with Venus in Aquarius reports that they all were very rebellious and often tried to get out of participating in family functions or duties. All had a need to escape the ordinary and felt aloof from and untouched by people’s expectations of them. One brother was fond of stirring up any conventional gathering with shocking comments or jokes. (AI)

A woman who had been married to a Venus in Aquarius man for twenty years, from whom she was now separated, wrote: “I still do not know how he feels about me. He says he loves me but his
actions say he is indifferent or antagonistic toward me. He is aware that I am now living with another man, but he has never asked me about him or questioned me about our arrangement. And yet he’s friendly to both of us” (AQ).

A number of interviews revealed that women with this planetary position engage in very little phony role-playing and also confirmed the relatively nonsexual orientation to many of their relationships—even if the relationship includes sexual involvement. Total detachment from their body or sexual nature was often mentioned. One person said, “It is as if they have to put up with sex to have the relationship they need in order to avoid loneliness.” Another man said about a Venus in Aquarius woman: “I could tell she was feeling closer to me when she started to talk about all her favorite authors!” (AI)

Debbi Kempton Smith throws in a few barbed comments as she discusses Venus in Aquarius, but there are within her quips some core truths and insights: “These dear sweet souls wonder what goes wrong in their relationships.… Their take-it-or-leave-it attitude might intrigue you at first, but these eminently beddable folks never seem to warm up emotionally unless you are not there.… Both sexes want romance to be unexpected, on the whim of the moment. They are most turned on when you don’t want to be turned on.… The women can take the whole romance thing or leave it, so their men cheat on them, desperately looking for emotion, and the women can take a deep breath and have ‘space’ to potter about with their weird hobbies” (SSN).

Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

Venus in the Water Signs

Venus in any of the Water signs connotes an extra degree of sensitivity and intensity of feeling response to all life experiences, especially in intimate, deeply personal exchanges with others. One of the most meaningful pleasures of life for these folks is feeling truly close to another person through shared sympathies—and sometimes through a sense of merger into a common emotional state, mood, or feeling a romantic ideal. A silent blending of feelings is much preferred to flamboyant or public demonstrations. They do tend to be hypersensitive and sometimes therefore overreact to stimuli of all sorts, especially to any personal attack or criticism. All three of these Venus signs are thus emotionally vulnerable; however, that obvious openness should not be interpreted as weakness, since there is almost always a surprising strength deep down in these people, once they are made to rely upon it through taking emotional risks. (The reader should also refer to the corresponding section in
Chapter 8
 to review some of the essential, but subtle, aspects of Venus in this element.)

Other keys to understanding Venus in the Water signs are as follows:

For these people, the physical body is a conduit for expressing and experiencing concentrated emotional energy interchange. Appearances matter to them less than to Venus in the other three elements, so long as the sharing
feels
 right.

Their deep emotional yearnings—which are often unconscious—motivate much of their approach to interpersonal life, and their
lack of certainty about their needs and goals allows room for such contradictory expressions as: instinctive privacy and secrecy needs, as well as a natural empathy and emotional involvement with others; intense selfishness and self-protectiveness, as well as a capacity for healing compassion and caring for others.

If Venus in Water signs gives in to negativity and oversensitivity, their seeming care for others can become merely a way of protecting themselves and avoiding hurt. In that case, they live so within themselves in a hidden world of irrational and often fearful imaginings that the capacity to reach out to others and to use their natural healing gifts can be blocked and even completely stagnate.

Venus in Cancer

Those with Venus located in the Moon sign Cancer experience fluctuation of the emotions that reflects their moods, which can be a gift manifesting as the expression of comforting kindness and affection to many, or as an annoying chronic surrender to fear and self-centered insecurity. They can be loyal, reliably sympathetic, and responsive to others’ needs; or they can be so insecure and fearful that they become timid, possessive, defensive, and somewhat paranoid. They can be childlike in a charming way, or childish in the selfish sense. The fear of being hurt causes some of these folks to keep a protective shell around themselves, which severely blocks their natural sensitivities and capacity for intimacy. This is the most domestic placement for Venus, and having the security of a home base, or at least a continual contact with the hometown or family they come from, is essential for their happiness. Whether it be a traditional family or merely a familiar place or group of people, they need an emotional base of operations. They usually love domestic activities and are often at their happiest doing the simple things of life—cooking, gardening, housecleaning,
light socializing while music plays. These folks are very sensitive to the atmosphere and to other people’s moods; perhaps because they themselves are vulnerable and easily hurt, they seek to soothe others’ feelings. This instinctive responsiveness to the feelings of others makes them extraordinarily likable, and this ability to tune in to others’ needs and moods makes them very good at teaching, sales, and business transactions. Their careful attention to detail (ensuring that their own security is enhanced by feeling certain that little can go wrong), their innate cheapness, and a dislike of waste, second only to that of Scorpio, also contribute to a natural ability to build a profitable business. Those with this placement exhibit a simplicity of manner and personal taste; they need artistic pleasure in their lives, although usually of a traditional, rural, or non-modernistic kind, and they are especially sensitive to music.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 C
ANCER

The way I have distilled the core psychological qualities of Venus in Cancer after almost four decades of working with astrological psychology is the following:

Expresses affection sensitively, comfortingly, protectively, and tenaciously.

A need for closeness may be hampered by moodiness, timidity, stinginess, or overly self-protective feelings.

Needs to nurture and be nurtured and to feel part of a family in order to be comfortable; very receptive, easily reflects others’ pleasures and moods.

Men and women with Venus in this lunar sign are old-fashioned about love and exceedingly protective of anyone they are attached to. Their images of love and marriage are quite traditional and usually center around a family home or activities that conjure up feelings of
warmth, closeness, and security. They are more compassionate than passionate—particularly the women. Their expression of caring is especially protective and usually motherly, and sex is essentially more emotional sharing than physical expression. Because these people fear ridicule or rejection, they take a while to develop the nerve to take any emotional risk. Being hugged and touched can encourage them to relax into an atmosphere of safe acceptance and can help cement a budding relationship. One does find in both sexes with Cancer Venus a tendency toward unclear, vacillating feelings about particular relationships, resulting in confusion, escapism, and, in some instances, infidelity and even complex threesome relationships. There is also often an ambivalence and evasiveness that detract from the ability to provide real dependability and commitment to the partner.

Venus in Cancer women often exemplify archetypal feminine personality traits and domestic instincts: cooking, mothering, homemaking, sewing, and other creative activities. Very clinging, they like to have their partner at home and prefer not to be alone. Yielding and adaptive to the partner’s needs and desires, they remain very receptive and sensitive so long as they do not feel betrayed, ignored, or abandoned. There is often a problem if the partner outgrows them through his own inner or intellectual development. They love to hold the beloved and also to be held. Sex is wrapped up with emotional security and confirms their commitment.

Men with Venus in Cancer are often favorites with women because they are some of the rare males who are content with simplicity and comfortable with expressing sensitive responsiveness and domestic skills. Their natural expression of receptivity to emotions and concern for others’ feelings seems so unique to women that they are immediately charmed. However, as a long-term partner, this fellow may not always be so intriguing as he seemed initially, since the archetypal image of a woman that he projects on a mate is the traditional mother-homemaker-cook upon whom he eventually tends to rely for
emotional expression and security needs. Much therefore depends on her own tastes and companionship needs as to whether that will be satisfying enough for her.

Some of the most perceptive insights into Cancer Venus I have found are these quotes from writings and questionnaires:

“Men with Venus in Cancer are usually fairly domestic and enjoy working around the house to make it even more beautiful. Many of them have ‘green thumbs’ and are good with plants and gardening” (AQ).

Those with Venus in Cancer, as Debbi Kempton Smith points out, “pull out all the soppy old romantic tricks. Act strong but tragically in need of care and feeding. The women want you to look like a banker or a North Woodsman in flannel shirt and beard.… Seduce them with the food of their own country. They can’t take a diet of foreign fare.… They have many moods, experimental minds, and will play baby one day and parent the next” (SSN).

One questionnaire respondent who has known five men with Venus in Cancer remarked that “all are great cooks and have a real home-loving personality. All are involved in long-term satisfactory relationships” (AQ).

Another woman noted that “men with Venus in Cancer can be surprisingly disloyal and even distant. Perhaps this stems from the self-protective nature? I’m struck by how surprisingly impersonally it can be expressed.… I observe a very generalized principle of caring. They take care of your needs, listen to you, etc., but it seems like they do so out of their own need to be protective or to take care of others rather than out of concern for you personally. In many instances, too, I’ve observed the individual with this placement to be attached to the mother (or a ‘mother figure’) or to have a mother who is very attached to them, very protective and/or domineering.
Also I’ve seen in several instances a marked sympathetic response in those with Venus in Cancer, the kind of person who will actually cry for joy because of someone’s kindness or out of sadness on hearing a story of someone they don’t even know” (AQ).

Venus in Scorpio

If Venus is in the intense, passionate, compulsive sign of Scorpio, one finds a person who never does anything half way. In fact,
thoroughness
 is one of the central features of their psychological and emotional nature, not just in close relationships but in all areas of life—financial, professional, and so on. They have to experience everything
completely
, and they intuitively sense if there is a missing piece to any puzzle or story; they always seem to know if something is “not quite right.” Their subconscious mind does all the work and analyzes the entire picture, and the more the individual learns to trust those intuitions, the better off they usually are. Scorpio has perhaps the most accurate “gut feelings” of all the signs of the zodiac (although they need to be wary of paranoia!), and that is why they can be great detectives, researchers, investors, or psychologists. This is a placement of emotional extremism; reactions can be violent and feelings are profoundly deep. And yet (humorously enough to others who find their emotional intensity quite obvious as they turn red with rage or jealousy), Venus in Scorpio thinks they are not revealing how strongly they feel. They can be unswervingly committed and emotionally loyal allies, as well as fierce and unforgiving enemies to those who betray them or offend their sense of justice or honor. In response to an offense, they can attack with vengeance, as if that will remedy their hurt or sense of injustice. These folks actually have little interest in or tolerance for most social activity, which they consider superficial and a waste of time; in addition, they are so acutely sensitive to social environments that they prefer to be alone most of the time. In fact, they do not trust
anything
 on
the surface of life: conventional behavior and posturing, the status quo social power structures, fashion or trendy appearances, or most other people. I think it is this extremely cautious approach to life, based on not trusting outer forces, that makes this sign so conservative (a word not usually used to describe Scorpio)—in the sense that they are alert to and actively dislike most intimations of change that they did not initiate. But when they do commit to change with a sense of mission, they can also transform and even revolutionize many areas of life with surprising energy and commitment. If one can handle their depth and intensity, one can find that being in a relationship with a Scorpio Venus person can make life far richer, and reveal many of its secrets.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 S
CORPIO

Even though so deep as to be at times unfathomable, I think the following core psychological qualities for Venus in Scorpio are an accurate distillation:

Expresses affection intensely, passionately, obsessively, with extreme, consuming feelings.

Social and love needs can be hindered by an inclination to secrecy and a reluctance to trust others.

Needs to penetrate deeply into a relationship with intense emotional power; give-and-take with others can generate a healing, transformative energy.

Men and women with Venus in Scorpio crave a healing, transformative experience through love and sex; but too often their emotions go to extremes of paranoia and jealousy, and the potential for healing is lost. They love the emotional intensity of love and sex, and being overwhelmed by the powerful energy polarities of life. Yet they also sometimes have a powerful repulsion toward sex because they love to be in control and hate anything that dominates them. In any case, Venus in
Scorpio is always fascinated by sex—its beauty, power, and mysteries. This is one reason those with this placement (like those with Scorpio Mars) often exhibit a somewhat promiscuous inclination, whether or not they act it out. Often, the individual will pursue a variety of experimental experiences during one period of life, and then exert strong discipline over the passions in the following phase—sometimes accompanied by pouring those intense energies into a new consuming obsession: psychological growth, meditation or yoga, a new business or career, and so on. Perhaps the greatest quality of Scorpio Venus is
passion
, but this should not be understood solely as sexual intensity. At their best, those with Venus in Scorpio demonstrate a passionate commitment to certain values, as well as a dedication to the beloved that can be powerfully inspiring. At worst, their selfish defensiveness based only on their keen survival instincts will prevent them from ever opening up and sharing with another person—thus resulting in years of manipulative behavior and, ultimately, in loneliness. In any case, these people are secretive and will not appreciate an intimate’s babbling to others about their private life. They have less tolerance for silly, superficial flirtation and game-playing than any other Venus sign. They regard it as a violation of their deepest emotions, for some regard sexual experience as an opportunity to learn and to be transformed at a profound level.

Women with Scorpio Venus often have a magnetism that men can feel across a large room. And these women enjoy knowing they can exert such a pull. They have an intense sexual pride. But just being attractive is not enough; they want to be
desired
. They also want action, not just talk or grand gestures. Silent communication through intense sharing of passionate feelings feeds them. As one man wrote in a questionnaire reply about women with this position, “They are crazy about sex and love, can get very attached sexually and can be permanently in love with someone” (AQ). They are usually quite experimental and
uninhibited in sexual behavior, unless something quite conservative (like Virgo) is strong in the chart.

Men with Venus in Scorpio tend to have a silent aura of power, mystery, and sexuality. They want total commitment from the partner, and they want it shown regularly. Two women that I interviewed described being involved with a Scorpio Venus man as a total, uncompromising commitment: “He demands my entire attention and wants lots of demonstrative affection—he doesn’t want to share me with anyone.” “He is so attuned to me that he’d know instantly if I had an affair.” This type of man is particularly attracted to women who exude intensity, depth, vitality, and erotic power—the subtler the better (rather than the Fire element type of extroverted glamour).

Some especially revealing insights into Scorpio Venus are found in the following quotes from writings, interviews, and questionnaire responses:

Julia Parker illuminates the picture further in her perceptive book
The Astrologer’s Handbook:
 “Here is a deep intensity of feeling which needs full expression and a very understanding partner. The individual will work enormously hard at his or her permanent relationship, to make it work for both partners, but sometimes the intensity is too great, and the partner can feel a little claustrophobic and need a less cloying and freer lifestyle” (AHB).

A woman with Scorpio Venus stated, “I hate people who judge others’ sexuality and sexual behavior. They don’t know how important it is to some people or what function it has in their lives. I have been fighting accusations since I was thirteen years old” (AI).

Mary Coleman concisely paints a picture of Venus in Scorpio and alludes to how
consuming
 that approach can be: “Responses to love and affection are reckless, highly charged and directed towards absolute conquest. In love, as in war, there are no compromises,
no second thoughts, no frivolities. But later, some recipients may discover they have lost not only their hearts but themselves as well” (PIC).

One woman (age fifty-three) contributed quite an illuminating response to the questionnaire: “I’ve known four men (ages thirty-two, forty, fifty-one, and fifty-nine) who have Venus in Scorpio. All have Sun in Libra and so are more artistic than many other Scorpio Venus men; three are professional musicians and one is a writer. All are very charismatic and magnetic, and all are
very
 aesthetically sensitive—repulsed by filth, ugliness, etc. They enjoy appearing mysterious and are constantly aware of attractive females. If a pretty girl pays attention to them, they glow and blow their ‘cool,’ which is obvious to everyone observing. Any mention of their past romantic encounters is accompanied by a profound sparkle in their eyes” (AQ).

Another woman contributed this reply to the questionnaire: “Venus in Scorpio people are secretive, very emotional and moody. The women are very powerful and aggressive, also possessive. There’s an undercurrent of desire to dominate, which can get out of hand when they are frustrated. When they are happy with themselves, though, they’re very attentive and supportive emotionally to others and very protective” (AQ).

Venus in Pisces

When Venus is located in the dreamy, idealistic, romantic sign of Pisces, it often takes many experiences and many years to clarify what kind of love and what type of relationship one wants. Some of these folks find that, since no one mortal is an adequate recipient of their emotional energies, they have to pour them into some religious or spiritual practice and/or devote themselves to a healing or service
profession in which they can in fact show love to a wide variety of people. They are often gifted with natural healing, soothing powers that help them dissipate other people’s fears, worries, and tensions. They can also find an appropriate mode of expression through one of the arts. A romantic idealism colors their vision of life, and good judgment about people is not inborn. They can, however, develop it with experience, especially if they have some practical features in their birth chart. In their early life, they often feel a rather impersonal, sentimental affection for everything and everyone. They feel, “I love everyone, so why choose?” and so they have difficulty committing themselves to any real, responsible relationship. In addition, the duality of Pisces (two fish swimming in opposite directions being the ancient symbol) further complicates matters: spiritual love versus the worldly love of an individual; and idealistic yearning versus an inclination toward always taking the easy way out through escapism, or even losing oneself through self-destructive behavior. Although Pisces Venus wavers between self-centeredness and selflessness, they usually feel a kind of generalized compassion for all suffering beings, human and animal. Their natural empathy and sympathy help lift them out of the self-pity that often afflicts them and is their biggest trap. Through service to others or to a higher ideal, they often transcend their own sense of worthlessness and confirm for themselves the value of their inner emotional and spiritual essence.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 V
ENUS IN
 P
ISCES

Although I have known many Venus in Pisces people over the last nearly forty years, it is still difficult to generalize about their core psychological qualities, but the following tendencies tend to be reliable:

Expresses affections sensitively, kindly, compassionately, and sympathetically; capable of selfless giving and adapting to others’ emotional needs.

Escapism, evasion, and confusion can undermine the ability to give of self and to receive from others; lack of discrimination can hamper forming solid relationships.

A need for a magical and romantic harmony; idealizes loved ones and love itself. Desires can be vague and unfocused, thus heightening vulnerability.

Because Venus in Pisces needs to
devote
 their affections toward some relationship and/or channel of expression but yet tends to be rather evasive in their emotions, one-to-one relationships tend to be an endlessly fascinating but also eternally perplexing area of their lives. They are easily swayed through their sympathies, and thus they can delude themselves into impractical relationships that have little hope of benefiting them and that even can be quite self-destructive. Because they naturally prefer not to see the faults, weakness, or decadence of their partner, they can be dreamily romantic, give everything emotionally, but later realize that the partnership was largely based on negative motivations: pity, fear, or obligation, sometimes based on their own desperation. They even have trouble falling in love with people unless they feel somewhat sorry for them. The independent, strong types leave them cold.

Women with Venus in Pisces are receptive and yielding to the other’s will and desires. They are not particularly sexual (unless other chart factors indicate it) since the physical exchange is a
channel
 for romantic and emotional feelings. The fact that many are not spontaneously sensual in a
physical
 way is one reason they like mind-altering substances that allow them to get into their body and senses. They need to base a relationship on shared
positive
 ideals—spiritual, artistic, humanitarian. Then, they can be appreciated for their vast capacity for affection and complexity of emotional expression. At best, they can give others courage through their unstinting support.

Men with a Pisces Venus are naturally attracted to someone who
is very sensitive and emotional, sympathetic and devoted. A person who is hard to pin down and rather “mystical” would arouse their interest. They are often interested in artistic types as well—poetic or musical people with delicate sensitivities who express idealistic yearnings in life.

Further insights into the hard-to-read Pisces Venus personality are illuminated in the following quotations, interviews, and questionnaire replies:

One woman wrote about Venus in Pisces men: “Very sensual, strong pleasure orientation. Tendency towards decadence. Wants to be really devoted to a sexual partner, but at the same time wants to love everyone” (AQ).

Debbi Kempton Smith describes the attitude Venus in Pisces people often have toward lovers: “Venus in Pisces will make a million excuses for you, and they’ll love you in this and the next ten thousand lives, but they’ll leave you when they run out of excuses for any cruel behavior on your part—
or if they see you deliberately trashing your life away”
 (SSN).

Grant Lewi fills out this portrait even further: “Your judgment of people isn’t too good; this is because your deepest interest is in your emotions for their own sake, and secondarily in the person on whom they are fixed. As a result they frequently fix on someone not especially interested in you, and you may lavish a lot of emotion and attention before you find out your love is not reciprocated.… You must learn to estimate others better in order to get back from the world some fair return for all the devotion you give to it” (AFM).

A thirty-seven-year-old Venus in Pisces woman remarked: “For years I was an easy lay. I liked how it enabled me so quickly to enter into emotionally deeper rapport at the kind of level people don’t usually reveal. It was my way of doing healing work” (AI).

Julia Parker emphasizes that these folks need to realize the value of how much they have to give and states: “There is in this placing a wealth of feeling and a warm sensitivity which will be directed towards the partner in a kind, loving way. A tendency to worry unduly over the lover is common, as are feelings of inadequacy and a constant need to do even more for them” (AHB).

Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

13

Mars in the Fire Signs

When Mars is in any Fire sign, the person usually has an innate capacity for powerful self-expression and self-projection into the outer world of activity in a way that makes a noticeable impact. Confident enthusiasm and dynamic initiative are often expressed instinctively. There can be born leadership qualities, inspirational abilities, and an endless stream of creative ideas and visions. But there can also be an innate sense of superiority that turns off others, and seems abrasive and self-centered. Mars in Fire signs individuals of both sexes usually tend to
order
 the other person around and to view the partner’s sexual feelings in an impersonal, mechanistic way. The field of close relationships is thus not generally the sphere where these folks excel (except in their own minds!), partly because they are so filled with their own elaborate schemes and pleasures that they don’t “reach out” to others and rarely bother to listen to their partners. A close relationship is often regarded (even though they may deny this) as simply another, often minor part of their vast, dramatic vision of life—a vision of which they are extremely proud, and which the prospective partner has either to join unreservedly or reject outright. Fire sign Mars people express all their aims and desires (including sexual) with a marked directness, and other people usually know where they stand about most issues. Sexual encounters are usually viewed as simple, straightforward demonstrations of personal power that should proceed in a direct way (A, B, and then C); and so, although these folks often exhibit celebrational and sporting attitudes toward sex, they are sometimes crude and not known for subtlety or sensitivity in erotic
exchanges. (The reader should also review my comments on Mars in Fire in
Chapter 8
 for further explanation of Mars in this element.) Other keys to understanding Mars in the Fire signs are as follows:

When the planet of aggression (Mars) is in the element of Fire, the entire world is their field of endeavor. However, in the intimate world of personal sharing and deeper emotions, their very power and insensitivity is their greatest problem. They can be sexually impatient and exhibit a rather “masculine” sexual approach that wants to reach a goal and validate their image of themselves, rather than seeing sexual interaction as an opportunity for give-and-take with the partner. People who take sex more seriously (for example, Earth and Water sign placements of Mars and Venus) may find the light enthusiasm of a Fire Mars disconcerting and personally unsatisfying.

Very proud and concerned with style, these folks demand
respect
. If insulted or offended, they can react with immediate loud and disdainful abuse that is surprisingly fierce. They project an image of much greater high-mindedness and morality than their behavior sometimes demonstrates.

Mars energy being expressed in a fiery way can be a most enjoyable, exuberant experience of high spirits and free giving of one’s self—so long as everything is going well automatically. But how the person deals with conflict, disappointment, or lack of quick satisfaction is the real test. How they cope with this test determines much about the ultimate success of their relationships and their entire lives.

Mars in Aries

Those with Mars in the quintessential Mars sign of Aries almost invariably express a physical restlessness and all-encompassing desire
for quick action—and also fast results (not always the best approach in the area of intimate relationships!). Most people with this placement exhibit some degree of brashness; they are very direct and blunt, which can be irritating or charmingly refreshing. In fact, they are often themselves easily irritated, since they are quick to anger or to show displeasure. But it is not usually personal nor long-lasting—just blowing off steam due to having to deal with any delaying encumbrances. These folks are always competitive, whether or not they admit it. There is usually a liking for sports, and often active participation. But in all areas of life, these folks need to be first if not best. They love pioneering into new fields and in fact cannot live without the excitement of constantly being challenged by something
new
. Rather impulsive but innately confident (they rarely stop long enough to doubt themselves), they focus their abundant energies on achieving their own desires, no matter what the challenges. If they fail, they bounce back rather quickly and then take on another new activity. Perhaps the most under-appreciated talent that the sign Aries demonstrates (a trait that is rarely mentioned in astrology books and applies to certain other Aries planets in addition to Mars) is their rapid and intuitive grasp of the essentials of any situation or difficulty. Such quick problem-solving ability is one reason they are often self-employed, or at least they try to have a work arrangement with a maximum of independence; for they
know
 what is required in many instances and do not have the patience for long meetings with people who have no clue. Headstrong and abrupt, their charm will not get them far in life! But their fearless and enthusiastic focus on the goal and their honest communication tend to win them respect. They hate routine, and the only thing they fear is boredom.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 A
RIES

Over the years I have distilled the core psychological qualities of Mars in Aries in the following way:

Asserts self competitively, directly, impatiently, and sometimes recklessly.

Intuitively grasps the essentials; restless urge for action directed toward new experience; often has entrepreneurial flair and/or mechanical ingenuity.

Sexual drive and physical energy are expressed impulsively, powerfully, and confidently.

Both men and women who have Mars in Aries have an attunement to a direct, focused physical energy, without the complexity found with Mars in many other signs. Mars here is an instinctual, impulsive energy. Aries tends to see only what is right in front of them, and when the object of their love or sexual interest is gone, the intensity of desire is diminished as well. The great advantage for those interacting with these folks in a relationship (or potential relationship) is that what you see is what you get. They are so bluntly obvious that they don’t even try to hide or falsify their motivations. Because of the Aries addiction to the
new
, these people have been described in many books as insatiable for sex or love affairs. Let’s just say they do need stimulation and that boredom and routine are fatal for them. Much depends on whether their energies are being channeled into other challenging areas. If they do not have a constructive outlet for their abundant physical energy, they can be overly aggressive, endlessly dissatisfied, or even quite hostile in relationships or sexual interactions. They deeply need a sport, physically active hobby, outdoor activity, or other challenging enterprise that will help them relax and dispel some of the famous Aries impatience. A very important feature of the Aries Mars personality (and true as well for Aries Venus and Sun)—one that has a direct impact on all their relationships, not just intimate ones—is their headstrong tendency to insist that their will and thoughts are
right, and not to listen whatsoever to the other person’s point of view. Need I say more?

Women with an Aries Mars take the initiative in many areas of life, including relationships and sex. They hate being ignored and insist on recognition for their abundant gifts and formidable character. Women whose Mars is in Aries not only express the aggressive qualities of Aries but also are attracted to men who exemplify those qualities. Her Mars image is dynamically activated by someone who is independent, self-motivating, adventurous, and straightforward. These women are impatient for action and can have quite a temper. Although they have plenty of sexual pride and demand inordinate respect, they do not really play demure feminine games very well; they are not passive, but rather initiate action and take charge. They are often “one of the boys” in their comfort with sports, strenuous outdoor activity, and the competitive worlds of business and career. They have a very focused “masculine” type of sexuality, often wanting lots of action but not caring to prolong sexual contact for emotional reasons. They see sex as physical motion and pleasurable tension release, rather than as an emotional merging or sharing. The women with this Mars position are generally impatient with males and expect them to be mechanically efficient but not to need much emotional support or affectionate encouragement.

Men who have an Aries Mars are in tune with the aggressive male archetype, and in relationships they show it by acting like cavemen! But seriously, folks, I’m told they can make very dashing lovers so long as the chase is on; but then they either get bored or begin to focus on achievement in some other new enterprise while the woman is expected to cater to their comfort. These guys are impatient in every field of activity, and so in relationships they prefer to do away with the romance and wooing requirements as soon as possible. They want to get to the point and therefore dive into a new relationship with
gusto and keep pouring it on until they are sure they have “won” the fair damsel. After that, they often give out the message, “Don’t try to change me. I am real, although maybe somewhat old-fashioned and crude. And now I’m going back to work.” Their energy level in relationships and sex, according to a number of interviews and questionnaires, vacillates tremendously—depending on how stimulated they are by something new.

A number of other insights into the nature of Aries Mars are evident in the following quotes from interviews, various writings, and questionnaire replies:

The following excerpt from a woman’s questionnaire reply touches on the extremism that one also sometimes sees in Aries Sun or Aries rising: “Mars in Aries women are usually very strong, self-centered individuals; very aggressive and willful. They know what they want and they go after it, and have a tendency to see most situations in terms of black and white, or either/or situations. They’re not halfway about anything. Sometimes they are almost brutal in their insistence upon having their own way” (AQ).

Mars in Aries women are further described in this quote from an interview with a fifty-three-year-old woman: “Though not usually mean-spirited, they can obliviously trample others in their path in their desire to be first or to get what they want. They are comfortable being the boss” (AI).

One woman with Mars in Aries expressed in an interview the characteristic speed and impatience for which Aries Mars is known: “I used to suffer from the female equivalent of premature ejaculation. My sexuality is so innocent, direct, quick—bam and it’s over” (AI).

The themes of independence and taking risks (in business, gambling, and personal affairs) pervade the traditional meaning of the
sign Aries, and this is all the more so for Mars in Aries, as this respondent commented: “The women seem to have the ability to do for themselves—very independent. In many cases, they seem forced to develop a lifestyle almost totally independent of their partner. Often they spend long periods separated from their mate. Men with Mars in Aries take a lot of chances and have a daredevil sort of attitude” (AQ).

Grant Lewi adds some original observations: “You feel best, act best when you are not limited or restricted by elders, superiors, authority of any kind. You are swift to anger at any intrusion on your personal rights.… Even in the chart of an otherwise timid soul, this will lend scrappiness to the nature” (AFM).

Mars in Leo

If Mars is in the extroverted, demonstrative sign of Leo, the individual is proud of his or her generosity and loyalty in relationships of all kinds. These folks’ big-hearted displays of affection and support can win them lifelong loyalty from others. Or—if their energies manifest as an exaggerated tendency to always be the center of the universe and to “lord it over” others—they can be excessively demanding of attention and overly dominating in their attitudes toward others. These folks have a fondness for “living life to the fullest” and delight in participating in group events, social rituals, and robust, physical activities of all kinds. They usually have a rather big personality with a dramatic flair and a friendly nature, a knack for telling stories and jokes, and an instinct for entertaining and encouraging others. In most people this placement bestows an impressive leadership ability, or at least a capacity to energize people or any enterprise through enthusiasm and a vision of positive possibilities. They are often good at sales professions, and they have a unique capacity to
vitalize
 organizational
efforts; even if the challenges are substantial, their determination is impressively steady and confident. They tend to be truthful and direct and also expect a lot of honesty in return. They do like to be in control, and they usually maintain an impressive dignity even in difficult situations—unless their considerable pride is offended to the point of their losing their temper, resulting in a surprising display of indignant anger. They are especially set off by any experience of humiliation.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 L
EO

The way I have distilled the core psychological qualities of Mars in Leo represents their overall tendencies:

Asserts self dramatically, warmly, expressively, confidently, and sometimes arrogantly; abundant vitality with a creative flair.

Need to assert oneself dynamically and creatively may be perceived by others as being pushy or domineering.

Physical and sexual energy is stimulated by attention and demonstrative generosity; needs to be complimented and appreciated for sexual, physical, or creative prowess.

Both men and women with Mars in Leo, because they are conscious of image and style, tend toward dramatic romantic gestures and are especially enamored of the intrigue, excitement, and rituals of partnering and mating. Because Leo Mars likes to be the boss or display authority in any situation, in discovering and experimenting with relationships they instinctively want to play the same role. At their insecure worst, they can be loud, pushy, and often obnoxious in their need to impress. Sex also has its dramatic, ritualistic attraction. With this Mars sign, the approach to sex—while not lacking in warm enthusiasm and appreciation for the partner—is
impersonal
. How can this be? I think it is because these people feel that, through sex, they are able to participate in a creative cosmic dance of life energy. They
exalt in that experience, but they sense that the creative energy itself is beyond the personal level. Both sexes with Leo Mars have quite a strong sex drive.

Women with a Leo Mars are invariably proud of their man. They like to shine in his glory and tend to build him up in public with compliments and praise. They are also conscious of how he dresses and the impression he makes on others. These women are particularly attracted to someone who is demonstrative, warm, and above pettiness. They often demand
devotion
 and can’t stand any form of humiliation. These ladies like to dress up, go out, be seen, and interact enthusiastically with friends and family. Women with Leo Mars have a powerful need to express appreciation to the male partner. But they also
demand
 much and dominate the partner by showing that no other woman could possibly boost his ego and self-respect enough to be a threat to her. Only if he falls off the pedestal of her idealized vision, through excessive pettiness, negativity, or worldly failure, will her loyalty be at risk.

Men who have Mars in Leo have an innate need to be noticed and like to be impressive. They would never come into a room or group meeting in a quiet, understated way, but rather tend to be publicly demonstrative. This also goes for their style with their partner; they like showing her off and even exaggerating their wonderful life. These guys definitely have charm. They can be warm, playful, and romantic, and are among the minority of men who actually
like
 going out a lot to parties, restaurants, etc. They like social activity, and they like being noticed out in public. Usually very self-conscious about appearance, they are more fascinated by clothes than most men, and many put much effort into staying in shape. Because they like to be “dominant,” they instinctively feel that a somewhat passive woman who endlessly admires them is the ideal. In the most extreme cases, they can be the ultimate male chauvinists. But in all cases, they tend to cultivate the image of themselves as the affectionate
givers
.

Some especially perceptive insights into Leo Mars are found in the following quotes from interviews, questionnaire responses, and other writings:

Julia Parker’s comments elaborate on some of the above observations: “The showing-off tendencies of Leo will be energetically expressed when Mars is in this sign. The individual will spend a great deal of enthusiasm and energy on making his or her presence felt, but will also have the happy knack of getting other people to do things. Here we have a team leader.… they can be somewhat bombastic and dogmatic at times” (AHB).

One contributor aptly remarked on their tendency to dramatize everything in life: “These people give anything they do a little extra, they give even the most mundane act a touch of personality.… They are the actors on the stage of life” (AQ).

Mary Coleman describes Leo Mars energies concisely: “Physical energies and passions are equally hefty, dynamic and filled with a lusty appetite for life. The sex drive is ‘all heart’ but liberally and constantly fueled by an indomitable will.… Less energetic spirits are scorched” (PIC).

As a forty-year-old woman who had had three relationships with Leo Mars men remarked, “Warm and sexy but at times quite overbearing. They tend to think they know what’s best for you; at times can try to steamroll others into doing what they want. Very susceptible to flattery, or to honest compliments. It gravitates their ego if someone pays a lot of attention to them, but I have seen some Leo Mars men who did not have a neurotic need for attention” (AQ).

A woman questionnaire respondent commented on the tendency of some Mars in Leo men to take chances in the game of life. She noted: “Men with Leo Mars are extremely capable in times of emergency, knowing just what to do. They are also real risk-takers” (AQ).

Mars in Sagittarius

When Mars is in the idealistic, future-oriented sign of Sagittarius, getting down to the everyday details of life and practical requirements of a relationship are not nearly so important as the professed long-term goals or large-scale vision that the person wants to fulfill. These people have a restless need for adventure, exploration, and high-spirited display of mental and physical independence. At worst, they can be rude and crude, boisterous and sarcastic in their opinions, and insensitive to how others react to their tactless, uncompromising statements. There is virtually always a deep-seated aspiration to some ideal in life, whether conscious or unconscious. They project themselves as higher-minded or more idealistic than the run-of-the-mill sorts of people who do not aim so high. In fact, although often humorous, good-natured, and tolerant in public expression, these folks are often unusually proud and suffer from the common Fire sign affliction: a sense of superiority. At their best, they are enthusiastic, positive thinking, and inspiring. They usually love outdoor activities and often have an extraordinary liking for animals. This reminds me of a unique facet of their nature: just as the centaur (the ancient symbol of Sagittarius) is the half-human archer on the half-animal horse’s body, the psychological and emotional nature of Sagittarius reflects a similar duality. The animalistic high spirits love freedom of action and instinct, but the human soul aspires to reach an ideal of morality or ethics. Their innate preference for broad-minded tolerance conflicts with their desire to be morally right, and their own behavior displays this inner struggle. Sometimes, this appears to others as a hypocritical tendency. For example, these folks love to preach the “truth,” regardless of how hurt or offended another person may be, yet they often cannot take hearing the truth about their own imperfections and can react with a fierce, indignant anger. In other words, they can be very outspoken
and judgmental if someone doesn’t measure up to their standards, but they cannot see what is right in front of them—namely, their own faults and shortcomings (which may be blatantly obvious to everyone else). Discretion is not a common virtue of this placement (nor of Sagittarius in general), for more than any other sign except Gemini, they cannot keep a confidence and often feel compelled to broadcast it—sometimes with an exaggerated embellishment to make it more important than it is.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 S
AGITTARIUS

Due to their tendency to be constantly changing, expanding their horizons, or expressing further plans for improvement in their lives, distilling the core psychological qualities of Mars in Sagittarius has been especially challenging:

Asserts self honestly, idealistically, energetically, impulsively, and tactlessly.

Decisiveness and strong actions are motivated by one’s aspirations toward an ideal or vision of the future, guided by one’s beliefs, morality, and inspirations.

Physical and sexual excitement is stimulated by adventurous activities; has an expansive urge for self-improvement and a restless need for exploration.

Men and women with Mars in Sagittarius are quite blunt and direct about sex, passion, and their opinions in general. They have an open-minded, enthusiastic attitude toward sex, and they expect their partner to demonstrate similar directness. However, because much of their approach to sex comes from their mental philosophy rather than from a grounded understanding of individual sexual complexity, they can often be among the most insensitive of all Mars signs toward other people’s sexual needs and their related emotions. They can be inadvertently
dismissive of others’ feelings, and their thoughtless, off-the-cuff, casual remarks can be experienced by others as quite painful. Because they instinctively
believe in
 complete freedom of expression, they are also usually quite lacking in moderation and modesty, which may turn off more traditional or aesthetically sensitive people.

Women with this Mars placement are especially happy to travel, pursue sports, or go on hikes, an affinity that makes them good companions for many types of physically active men. They like a direct sexual approach that strives to reach a goal, and they show little understanding and get quite irritated if their own target is not reached. Not being particularly sentimental, they then want it over and done with, and go on to the next activity. The woman with Mars in Sagittarius is also attracted to someone who can teach her something, or participate in her development, or help her to improve herself. She tends to demand high ideals and moral character from her partner, and she can lose considerable respect for him rapidly if his flaws show up as falling short of her standards of honesty and morality. She likes someone athletic, optimistic, and upbeat, who has definite expansive plans for the future.

Men with this Mars sign will try to impress the object of their desires with their jokes, good nature, honesty, and idealism. Showing the partner a good time is important to them, and they actually care if the potential partner is enjoying herself. Being supportive of her efforts at learning or self-improvement is also among his highest priorities, even if he is too pushy or self-righteous at times. This man will definitely not hesitate to expound at length on what he thinks and believes, and if what she hears leaves her cold, there’s little chance of the relationship progressing. Sexually, this guy is one of the most flexible (in attitude) of all Mars signs, and he can even now and then appreciate directness if the woman is not sexually interested at the time.

The following quotes from interviews, questionnaire responses, and other writings amply fill out the picture of Sagittarius Mars:

Further observations supporting some of my above comments are found in a woman’s questionnaire response: “People with Mars in Sagittarius are blunt, straightforward, and direct in their ways of doing things and sexually. They have a ‘tell it like it is’ attitude and a strong and very blunt sex drive. They approach all things with some measure of humor and an adventurous spirit. They can be very impersonal in sexual relationships but will generally be honest about their feelings in a typically Sagittarian way. Not too sentimental, they just lay it on the line and see what happens, with very little pretense” (AQ).

Mary Coleman fills out the picture: “There is always another escapade, another adventure over the greener hills or round the next corner. The sex drive is thus hearty but applies itself more to athletics than histrionics in achieving satisfaction—often with casual unconcern for the hour or the location” (PIC).

Regarding women with Sagittarius Mars, one man remarked: “They like to have their sex activity be very physical and can be quick to turn on sexually. They will move on quickly to a new relationship even though they may still be in love with the last person” (AQ).

Grant Lewi’s observations apply to the entire Sagittarius Mars method of operation, as well as to their relationship activities: “You love to generalize, and may be irked by the details of life; a love of order is not usual with this position of Mars and must be studiously cultivated. A kind of haphazard, slapdash way of going at things should be overcome.… Also, you have to be aware that, by keeping your eyes fixed on high and far-off things, you don’t miss the essentials of life” (AFM).

The duality of the Sagittarius Mars personality, reflected in the symbol of the half-man, half-horse centaur, comes through in this
description by a middle-aged man: “Talks a ‘moral’ line, like self-righteous politicians, but only moral when they’re getting regular, proper sex. But they’ll settle for ‘immoral’ dalliances when that’s all that’s available. In other words, they’re high-minded when it’s easy to be so, but often they later treat their ‘illicit’ lover like dirt (kind of like Leo does) when a more proper partner comes along” (AI).
