Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

14

Mars in the Earth Signs

Mars in any of the Earth signs denotes a steady, systematic, constructive, and often ambitious approach to getting things done. People with this Mars placement don’t seem to mind taking the “tried and true” (although somewhat tedious) pathways to worldly accomplishment, so long as their efforts will eventually result in practical goals being achieved. These folks instinctively understand the rhythms of nature and the human need for security and structure, not just in relationships but in every aspect of life. Caution, self-protection, practicality, and patience are the keynotes of their
modus operandi
, and they usually display a significant focus on efficiency and a real sense of satisfaction at achieving challenging goals that required persistence. Because they are concerned with public propriety, their sensuality is tightly controlled. Therefore, they do not readily reveal their desires or sexual attractions, nor do they admit (often even to themselves) the practical advantages they envision in establishing a particular intimate relationship. Although sensual, they are not necessarily romantic in the sense of easily losing themselves in emotions. (The reader should also refer to the corresponding section of
Chapter 8
 to review my previous comments on Mars in the Earth element.)

Other keys to understanding Mars in the Earth signs are as follows:

Because of the intensity of immediate physical presence that people with Earth Mars naturally express, those encountering them can experience the phenomenon of life and time
slowing down
, as it
becomes concentrated in the physical reality of the environment and atmosphere of the present moment.

External appearance and dress are important to most of these people, and their own perception of respectability (that is, being taken seriously) is closely associated with the physical image they project. Both sexes with this placement want to be
physically
 desirable.

Although Mars in the Earth element is very effective at getting things done in the world, in the field of close relationships, the efficient ability to
use
 things and people can be their biggest obstacle to building a rewarding long-term relationship. Possessiveness and/or using another person to achieve one’s own aims are, after all, not the best basis upon which to construct a successful relationship.

Mars in Taurus

When Mars is found in the sign Taurus, the aggressive urges are held under control but should never be underestimated, for they are stubbornly powerful and persistent. In fact, although Taurus is rarely described as “aggressive” in astrological literature, those with Mars (or the Sun) in the sign of the bull are among the most willful, purposeful, obstinate beings in the world in terms of unrelentingly pursuing their own desires and goals. At best, they are great builders or creators, industrious but also often with an artistic flair. (Taurus is a sign associated with Venus, the planet of art and beauty, so here we have Mars in a Venus sign, energizing both the erotic passions and broader aesthetic and sensual attunements.) Mars’ position always shows the
modus operandi
 (method of operation, or “MO” in some modern business books), and in slow, deliberate Taurus the pace is never hurried and there is a love of routine that can be seen as (and sometimes is) lethargy. However, to the surprise of many onlookers, their steady persistence of effort over a number of years quite often
yields considerable accomplishments. They are living examples of the old adage, “Slow but steady wins the race.” There is a streak of complacency, smug self-satisfaction, and even debilitating laziness in many of these people, but it’s usually periodic and dominant in only a relatively few of these individuals. And in my experience, those people are then extremely unhappy as they get older if they did not exert themselves to achieve something
concrete
 in life. In a few cases, I have seen severe self-hatred and bitterness develop as the substantial anger of Taurus Mars is turned inward. The unapologetic love of and indulgence in all sorts of pleasures of the senses are characteristic of Taurus, and those with Mars here pursue pleasure, and often luxury and possessions, with great fervor. They often crave “the finer things of life” and feel that indeed they
deserve
 them because they are obviously special and merit the best. They exhibit a great pride about adornments, clothes, money, and possessions; and they act childishly temperamental if their possessions are threatened. This sense of
deserving abundance
 that seems innate in Mars in Taurus (as well as in a number of other planets when located in Taurus) also leads to a chronic wastefulness that creates conflicts in relationships with those attuned to the more frugal signs, such as Scorpio, Cancer, and Aquarius.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 T
AURUS

The distillation of the core psychological qualities of Taurus Mars takes the following form:

Asserts self steadily, retentively, conservatively, and stubbornly.

Initiative and drive directed toward consolidation, productivity, comfort, and enjoyment of life’s simple pleasures; often a creative and/or artistic flair.

Physical energy and sexual drive are influenced by a deep appreciation of the physical senses; may tend toward complacency and laziness.

Men and women with Mars in Taurus both have a strong, consistent sex drive and powerful sensual urges. The physical world is ultimately real to them, and they have a strongly developed sense of touch. Thus this attunement adds a broad sensuality to sexual life and creates a need for regular security-enhancing demonstrations of physical assurance and desire. However, their deep, consistent sex drive is not quickly sparked (as in a Fire sign Mars) and is rather cautious in approach, never wanting to risk exposing oneself to ridicule or failure. That is the motivation for their propriety, and they will often decide to not “make a move” on a desirable person if the situation seems too out of the ordinary or potentially embarrassing. They are thus rather slow to arouse; but once the deeper desires are awakened, they seek to prolong the experience to an extent not appreciated by more impatient partners, who find such slow, sensually indulgent, predictable sex rather boring. Once they feel secure, they are often very demanding of physical attention, and their sexual attitude is quite fixed and selfish—more focused on “get” than “give.” One might summarize this position by saying that they are sexually quite traditional but very demanding once the partner has been settled on. In short, as much as possible they want to
have
 their source of pleasure fully secured and ensured.

Women with Mars in Taurus may wait a while before getting involved with someone, but once they do, their desires come out forcefully and often greedily. These women then want to be
wanted
 and can get very angry if their deep need for pleasure is not satisfied. Unfortunately, they rarely communicate verbally about their needs or desires with their partners, who may then feel used and thus want to distance themselves from such a powerful, consuming earthiness. These women often have an archetypal primitive streak in their sensuality, and they want to be dominated, even if they took Feminism 101 in college. They also want to be comfortable and taken care of materially. They rather like the traditional dominant male image, so long as the male in their lives is able to really fulfill that role.

Men with Mars in Taurus, in addition to showing the previously mentioned characteristics, want to demonstrate their power and attractiveness through providing material things and comfort for their desired ones. These guys can be charming but also very jealous and possessive—even to a fiercely irrational extreme. One of their gifts is patience. It may regularly frustrate others to no end, since almost no one is as patient as Taurus; but they can systematically achieve things in almost any field of endeavor that more restless, less determined people could not accomplish.

Some insights into Taurus Mars are found in the following quotes from interviews, various writings, and questionnaire replies:

Combining comments from an interview and a questionnaire that both touch on the same theme, two women described Taurus Mars men as follows: “Mars in Taurus men are very sensual and pleasure loving, but usually passive sexually. They like to be taken care of and pampered, and prefer women who are into this kind of attention giving. They’re conservative and not very experimental” (AQ). The following was said by a woman who was herself very earthy, with both Venus and Mars in Earth signs: “Predictable, inflexible, and content with the same routine each time. Eventually, they get dull” (AI).

Grant Lewi insightfully comments on how Taurus Mars goes about getting their way through what I might call a “negative cooperation approach.” It is not that they fight you or overtly disagree, but you are just forced to wait forever before they’ll give in to even considering another point of view: “Your motto is, ‘I don’t want to fight; just let’s do it my way.’ You are a master of the art of passive aggression and passive resistance; … you keep plowing along in the same furrow” (AFM).

Two comments on Mars in Taurus women form an interesting juxtaposition: A woman interviewee with Taurus Mars stated, “I like
men to have some physical
substance
, so I can grab them and feel they’re really here” (AI). A man wrote that Taurus Mars women “like you to treat them like a lady, even if they’re not” (AQ).

Mars in Virgo

When Mars is in the analytical, systematic sign of Virgo, the individual is noticeably industrious and devoted to accomplishing any assigned job. Individuals with Mars in Virgo can be ingenious at creating new ways of achieving the desired results in any field, paying attention to every detail that has an impact on the final product or project. Although their
modus operandi
 is different from Scorpio’s (Virgo’s being more mentally analytical, whereas Scorpio’s is intuitive), they resemble Scorpio Mars in their thoroughness and attention to all the various factors involved. Virgo itself is the sign of the
craftsman
, and people with this placement are willing to spend considerable time perfecting the finer details of the task at hand. As a Virgo Mars artist said in an interview, “The way I go about doing my art is extremely methodical. And my art is getting smaller and smaller, more and more detailed” (AI). Those with Virgo Mars are notoriously hardworking, to the extent that their jobs so dominate their lives and obsess their worrisome minds that little time remains for anything else, including healthy relationships. Because in fact these folks can do many jobs better than most because of their practical intelligence (and because they do not mind doing the dirty work that others often shirk), they often wind up in a vicious circle wherein their coworkers give them even more work, since the Virgo Mars folks have often said that no one else “does it well enough” anyhow. Problems arise because Virgo Mars is instinctively critical of those who do not use their own demanding, disciplined methods. Even when a Virgo Mars person is not super competent, they are picky and faultfinding, and tend to insist that others do it their way. Virgo Mars people seek order (and orderliness)
in all things, which is a valuable talent in many fields of work, precise research, or scholarship, although it is not the ideal guiding principle in emotional relationships. When the perfectionist streak is applied to individual human beings, it results in a tendency to want to
reform
 others; and these folks rarely seem to learn that criticism and pointing out defects are not the most effective ways to influence another person’s thinking or behavior.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 V
IRGO

The most precise way I can formulate the core psychological qualities of Virgo Mars is the following:

Asserts self modestly, helpfully, analytically, dutifully, sometimes with petty criticism.

Need to strive toward perfection in order to achieve desires; decisiveness and initiative may be hindered by self-criticism and over-attentiveness to details.

An underlying need to serve influences the physical energy and willpower; ability to work hard and vigorously with practical intelligence.

For both men and women with this placement, even their approach to sex works through the analytical mind. Everything is scrutinized and analyzed, questioned and worried about. As the majority of interviews and questionnaires emphasized, there can be a sort of clinical approach to intimacy and sex, leading often to a rather narrow interest in techniques or in books about sexuality research or surveys. They may study the subject so much that all spontaneity becomes impossible, since everything proceeds from a calculated mental plan rather than from deep feeling or passion. Mars in this Earth sign is quite sensual, but it also usually involves substantial mental activity. One solution seems to be finding a relationship wherein there is comfortable
sensuality combined with good verbal communication. Due to the sometimes excessive focus on daily work by these folks, in many cases much of the raw physical and mental energy is channeled into duties or sublimated into achievement. In both sexes, the sex drive generally seems to be moderate, and it is far less persistent in Virgo than in the other two Earth signs. In fact, some people with this placement acknowledge that they have virtually none. And if that is the case, they will feel much more relaxed if they simply accept the fact that this is their nature, rather than getting caught up in endless self-analysis. The hyperactive analytical mind does present a dilemma in relationships: if they focus it on themselves, they become tied up in knots of doubt, overly self-conscious, or depressed; if they focus it on the partner, it is rarely appreciated and often makes the other person understandably defensive. As Debbi Kempton Smith wrote about Virgo Mars, “You can take people apart, but you’re not too hot at putting them together again. They sense that you’re rating them on a scale of one to ten in a dozen different categories” (SSN). Admittedly, many people do play some kind of private rating game in relationships, especially in the early stages, but in my research more people with this Mars position than any other volunteered that they did in fact have a rating scale of some kind! Virgo is quite an introverted, nonaggressive sign position for powerful Mars, and this planet’s energy is therefore often turned inward, resulting in nervous tension and sometimes a sexuality that is slow to awaken. It is important for people with Mars in Virgo to learn how to relax, and to get out of their heads and into their quite sensual bodies; for then they can open up to experience the kind of sharing and affection that they especially appreciate, since they usually don’t have a great deal of self-confidence.

Women with Mars in Virgo are dynamically active when at work or performing the domestic duties or arts at which they also often excel, but they are usually quite passive in their approach to relationships and especially toward sex. They are very careful about involving the
sex energy and tend to keep a tight hold over it. Rarely very passionate (unless the Sun or some other major factor is in a more sexual sign), these women do like being desirable and appreciate being exciting to men. They seem to get far more pleasure, in fact, from seeing that they have been helpful or stimulating to a partner than they do from the physical or emotional pleasure they themselves experience. They do like hugging and overall sensuous contact that helps them get out of their minds and grounded back on the earth for a while. And in fact, using the healing gifts that a number of these women have in the field of therapeutic touch can also provide this healthy grounding, as well as mental satisfaction. They are especially attracted to a fairly conventional, proper man, and he absolutely has to appear
clean
 and self-disciplined. A noticeable show of discrimination and intellectual acumen are also attractive to these women.

Men with a Virgo Mars like to demonstrate their expertise and skills in various fields to impress a woman. They may also offer, or insist in giving, help in practical ways, whether or not the woman appreciates that particular kind of aid or seeks that kind of self-improvement. This is one of the rare Mars signs in a man that is often expressed with a certain humility, and his lack of a macho or arrogant attitude can be a real plus. Many men with this placement, however, do tend to worry about their sexual energy. Others tend to overestimate the breadth of their sexual experience or their knowledge or technical competence. One other behavior that I have observed in
both
 sexes, although more obviously aggressive in the men, is a pugnacious, bitingly critical defensiveness that seems rather easily aroused if their desires (sexual or otherwise) are thwarted.

To broaden this portrait of Mars in Virgo, the following quotes from interviews, questionnaire replies, and other writings are instructive:

One female questionnaire respondent wrote: “Mars in Virgo are the ones who clean everything, and some things are best left alone.
They are famous for reading
Masters and Johnson
 [statistical, laboratory studies of sexuality—SA] to their lover; yet somehow they convince themselves that sex is biologically cleansing. They are brazenly clinical and often tell you how well you did, along with keeping a scorecard. Somehow, that deflates their love life” (AQ).

An interview with a woman approaching thirty who had a significant relationship with a Mars in Virgo man yielded the following comments: “They seek to show their strength through service and patient helpfulness. This man was quite screwed up about sex and the madonna/prostitute conflict—the purity question, maybe because of the influence of the Catholic Church in his case. When rebuffed, he got surprisingly abusive, angry, sharp-tongued, and critical” (AI).

A quite astrologically knowledgeable man wrote regarding men with Mars in Virgo: “Craftsmanlike energy; usually have periods of total celibacy. They definitely bitch a lot!” (AQ).

Grant Lewi adds more general observations—not limited to the field of relationships—which I’m including since I find them extremely accurate: “Your energies are directed to system, or, in the negative manifestation, to no-system.… One way or another, your effort is related to the order of your life. You will be the most systematic, logical, orderly, precise person in the world. Or else you will live from hand to mouth, your rooms in disorder, in constant rebellion against putting things where they belong.” He continues with these suggestions: “Master detail, forget it, and then let your cleverness—of which you have plenty!—go to work on more essential things. The danger of this position is narrowness of effort through inability to see the forest for the trees …” (AFM).

Another woman’s questionnaire remarked about Virgo Mars men: “In my experience (which has not been wide but has been striking!)
Mars in Virgo is definitely
not
 asexual, but a sign of a great deal of endurance and technical expertise, although perhaps a little self-centered and a bit ‘show-offy’ in bed” (AQ).

Perhaps Tiffany Holmes sums up best the Virgo Mars dilemma in relationships when she writes: “Mars’ verbal expression may seem more civilized [than some other Mars signs—SA], although not to the sexual partner! This energy is best used in detailed professional work; militant perfectionism finds more rewards at the office … than in the bedroom. Intimacy and criticism coexist only in a state of truce, at best” (WA).

Mars in Capricorn

When Mars is located in the eminently practical, self-disciplined sign of Capricorn, the individual’s expression of ambition and self-assertion is notable for its patience and restraint—but also for its persistence and underlying physical strength. Individuals with Mars in Capricorn even have the ability to strengthen others through consistent support and practical advice. Although these folks do have almost an obsession about
authority
, different personalities manifest this underlying attitude quite differently: some are obviously ambitious to get to the top of whatever profession they pursue and work with notable determination (even ruthlessness) toward that goal; some assume that such an achievement will take years and even underestimate themselves when everyone else sees that they are the obvious choice to be “the boss” or to get the promotion; and a few pose obnoxiously as the ultimate authority without paying their dues, always needing to have the last word and to feel they are dominating everyone else, which alienates others, who may resent them or simply leave the room in disgust. The urge to have control and power (and thus safety) in all worldly affairs, and in relationships as well, pervades the motivations of Capricorn
Mars. Their approach is methodical—sometimes so overly planned as to completely lack spontaneity—and their attitude is almost always serious, although they often have quite a dry sense of humor. These folks actually
need
 and even love heavy responsibility and duty, and they carry it off well. In fact, looking important motivates them. They love a challenge, even if they do not know it consciously; for, as Julia Parker wrote, “A driving force is present, and those with Mars in this sign have some formidable resources to fall back on, especially when they experience difficult conditions” (AHB). And she adds an observation that I have also seen to be very true: “Demanding exercise is important for the physical well-being of a Mars in Capricorn type.”

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 C
APRICORN

After decades of working with and observing many people who have this placement, the most accurate distillation of the Capricorn Mars core psychological qualities can be expressed as follows:

Asserts self cautiously, seriously, authoritatively, ambitiously, with strong self-discipline.

Decisiveness is accompanied by careful planning, calculation, and patience. Physical energy is often directed toward personal material goals and long-term achievement.

Pursues desires steadily and persistently through conventional channels: sex urge is strong and self-controlled.

Both men and women with this placement have a deep earthiness and a sexual nature attuned to the archetypal physical instinct. They sense its insistence and power, and they therefore keep it under strict control until the proper time arrives to unleash their urges. Surprising to many others who see them as very remote and cold, they have a remarkable depth of passion and demonstrable desire, once the appropriate scenario is available. They also have deeply insistent demands,
for they know what they want. In fact, they take charge authoritatively in sexual relationships, and they can get quite raunchy in a way that may appear crude to people of more sensitive Mars and Venus attunements. They like being in control in everything, including love and sex. For these folks, sex is an instinct that has to be dealt with practically, and expressing their desires does not necessarily have anything to do with softness or sensitivity of emotions. If these qualities are indeed present, they emanate from other planetary placements in the birth chart. Mars in Capricorn is all about
control
, and these individuals not only strongly discipline the expression of their sexual energy in intimate interactions, but also can even go for years with no sex whatsoever … although they do not like it.

Women with Mars in Capricorn are noticeably strong and capable people, and that same description applies to their expression of sexual energy. Although often very conservative in public presentation and appearance, they have an insistent drive not only toward sex but also toward achieving respectability and a long-term commitment. In these women, many factors other than their instinctive passions and sensuality can open or close doors to their sexuality. As one woman with Capricorn Mars said in an interview, “I like to project an image of control” (AI). These women often
love
 the business world and the practical give-and-take it involves. They tend to be attracted to men who are aloof, successful, serious, and ambitious—the more reserved and inscrutable, the better.

Men with a Capricorn Mars are very cautious about asserting themselves and in sexual situations are particularly alert to any rejection or ridicule. They will immediately withdraw if they feel demeaned, and they have to feel respect and commitment from the partner in order for their full sexual energies to be expressed. Basically, it is hard for them to freely express desire or affection. But once they feel assured of acceptance, there is no end to their energy, since they generally have incredible physical stamina. These men are usually conscious of the
need to sell themselves to a woman based on what they can provide materially—and I don’t mean just sex.
Control
 is again the watchword, and these guys get insecure if they have to be too passive. They are very authoritative, impersonal, and a bit mechanical in their approach to sex and passionate love. They are usually quite conventional sexually; and most don’t take many behavioral or emotional risks in life or relationships.

Some particularly perceptive insights into the nature of Capricorn Mars are provided by the following interview quotes, questionnaire responses, and other sources:

One woman in an interview remarked about Capricorn Mars men that they “had to be absolutely in control and will not reveal emotion or any kind of vulnerability.” She described them as impersonal and even mechanical in some cases. (AI)

A man commenting on Capricorn Mars women in a questionnaire wrote, “… lustful and aggressive sexually; they like older men and are sexually manipulative” (AQ).

Mary Coleman describes these people’s nature in a way that I think cleverly catches some of their key characteristics: “Physical energies and passions are kept under rock-like restraint until shaken free by the proper person at the proper time in the proper place. Then the switch from sobriety to sensuality is as earth-shattering as it is unexpected. The sex drive is thus heavily repressed, but forever watching and waiting for the moment when it can throw off its load of inhibitions and find release” (PIC).

Another woman commented about men with this placement in an interview by describing them as “very ambitious—name-droppers and social climbers. And especially image conscious when considering marriage or any public partner” (AI).

A few extracts from Debbi Kempton Smith’s comments on Capricorn Mars hit the nail on the head: “… gloomy, but you are jolly good value as you are the height of responsibility in a relationship.… Love lasts a long time with you. Your power turns people on. You manipulate people, and yet it’s usually for their own good” (SSN).

Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

15

Mars in the Air Signs

When Mars is in any of the Air signs, a great burst of aggressive energy flows into mental and social activity. The nervous system gets hyped up, and verbal skills are usually evident at an early age. Self-assertion is usually verbal and interpersonal; hence, there is often a love of debate, argument, or intellectual competition. This can of course stimulate and nurture some kinds of relationships, while making other types of closeness quite difficult. But in spite of the clarity of ideas that may be presented so glibly in the abstract, the urge to act decisively
in one’s own life
 is often inhibited by endless analysis of theoretical options. Intellectual activity and communication are the keys to energizing these people, not only mentally, but also physically;
they have to be mentally engaged in an activity in order to have enough physical energy to accomplish it
. Their innate detachment (unlike the more impulsive Fire or Water Mars signs) makes them more cautious before taking action, adding to the surprising conservatism often seen in Air Mars people. I explain this in more detail in
Chapter 8
, which the reader should review. All the Mars placements in Air exemplify this detachment and tendency toward analysis in advance of decision, although Gemini and Aquarius are more experimental than the more cautious, serious Libra.

Other keys to understanding Mars in the Air signs are as follows:

Dynamic exchange of ideas is physically energizing.
Concepts
 are far more real than anything on the earthly plane and motivate these people’s desires and enthusiasms. Sharing with others who hold
compatible intellectual, social, or political views can also be particularly stimulating.

A genuine enthusiastic interest in other people as individuals is natural for these folks; and because they demonstrate such an obvious personal interest in others, their own aims and ambitions can be most easily realized through the help of or association with other people. Hence, sales or promotional activities, consulting or counseling, and teaching are common channels for Air Mars energies.

With Mars (the planet of sexual desire and stimulation) in the intellectual element Air, a remarkable number of these people make a mental study of sexual behavior, sexual types, and sexual categories. They are very curious, but more verbally and visually oriented than physically engaged. They often confuse their mental excitement with potential actions: as one male interviewee with Mars in Air told me, “I often don’t want sex when I think I want sex. All I want is grounding and physical affection in contact with the other person” (AI). In fact interviewees with Mars in all three Air signs specifically volunteered that they were becoming increasingly conscious of actually not being as interested in sexual activity as they had once thought. Evidently, social influences (to which Air signs are particularly susceptible) molded their assumptions and stimulated their curiosity about sex, although their innate energy attunement—depending on the rest of the chart—was often of a quite different kind.

Mars in Gemini

Those with Mars in Gemini invariably have highly developed verbal, social, and/or manual skills that can take them far in life. In fact, they are known for versatility and often have remarkable abilities in
multiple fields, a trait that can prevent them from focusing on any endeavor long enough to make it a valuable part of a long-term life plan. They have lively, endlessly curious minds that constantly seek to make
connections
 (perhaps
the
 Gemini keyword) between ideas, people, or experiences. Their mental energy seems boundless, and perhaps this is why they do not hesitate to take off on another new interest, trip, or relationship every few days. That very tendency, however, often leads them to fritter away their energies and talents on a myriad of petty details, superficial ideas, or pointless social activities.
Communication
 is another obsession with Gemini. Things go well on this level if they can tone down their rather aggressive way of asserting their own ideas, which can degenerate into argumentativeness or sometimes a “know-it-all” attitude. If they can avoid the tendency of putting their foot in their mouth, they can be very effective in any number of professions, such as sales and marketing, public relations, teaching, or literary pursuits. Ingenious, clever, inventive, and often mechanically or electronically minded, this is the first person who comes to mind when one wonders just who might know about some odd problem, detail, or solution—rather like a walking yellow pages. It is a point of pride to these folks that they have an infinite number of friends and acquaintances from all walks of life; and they love showing off their various buddies to others in their vast network. They also can become quite angry with those who are disinclined to waste time “meeting” lots of people they will never want to see again. Despite the strong emphasis on mental agility found in these folks, Mars in Gemini is also surprisingly common in accomplished athletes, since it gives quick reflexes, mental alertness, and—at its best—a harmonious alignment of the nervous system, mind, and hand-eye coordination. In fact, it is strongly advisable that all these people participate in physically active pursuits to counterbalance and relax the mental side of their nature.

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 G
EMINI

The core psychological qualities of Gemini Mars can be encapsulated as follows:

Asserts self verbally, flexibly, cleverly, communicatively, through a variety of skills.

The focus of one’s desires changes quickly and often; easily diverted. Expresses a wide-ranging friendliness; very good at making connections.

Physical energy and sexual drive are affected by mentally stimulating conversations, images, or curious new ideas; very open-minded.

Both men and women with Gemini Mars are easily distracted and often confused about what they really want in relationships. Their romantic and passionate interests can change quickly, partly because—just as with everything else in life—they are endlessly curious about sex and the games and stimulating exchanges of interpersonal dynamics. As Mary Coleman wrote, “The sex drive diffuses itself … blowing this way and that in the search for cleverer partners and more amusing liaisons” (PIC). In fact, these people need mental and verbal stimuli to keep their flames burning; thus, they will find a partner satisfying only if he or she also has a wide range of interests and a capacity for always learning something new, going somewhere different, or expressing a stimulating, original observation. Because much of the Mars energy here goes into mental activity, the sex drive is not usually particularly strong, at least not in a physical way. However, especially if certain other factors are emphasized in the birth chart, these folks can appear extraordinarily
interested
 in sex and fascinated by the varieties of eroticism.

Women with Mars in Gemini usually display manual or musical, if not intellectual, skills. There is commonly a youthfulness that makes
them, for example, good teachers of young people. Adaptable and flexible, they like variety and want to share many activities with a partner. They often would rather talk than be physically demonstrative, and they may prefer a party or a chat to an intense encounter. Habitually flirtatious, these women nevertheless often have trouble getting into their bodies when things progress past superficial demonstrations. Mars in Gemini women are attracted to someone who is clever, verbally oriented, amusing, and open to learning new things. Someone who keeps her guessing or who is particularly witty will be especially intriguing.

Men with Mars in Gemini are also known for flirtatiousness. One interviewee said that a Gemini Mars man she had been with “wanted everybody else too” and constantly flirted with almost anyone in sight. If, however, this type can settle down and focus on one person, he will want to impress her by showing how accomplished, skilled, or clever he is. There is often in these men a sort of boyish innocence, which can be charming, although it is also sometimes frustrating if one wants clarity of intention and real commitment. The frequent use of their hands to make contact with others seems to be characteristic of these men, and I do not mean just groping! They tend to be quite manually demonstrative with all kinds of friends of both sexes. After all, they need to make a
connection
 and if their words cannot do so sufficiently, a light contact with the hand perhaps can.

Some excellent insights into the nature of Gemini Mars are found in the following book, interview, and questionnaire quotes:

A thirty-year-old woman who had known four Gemini Mars men commented in a questionnaire: “Seem to be genuinely interested in the thoughts of others. These people want verbal feedback from others. One of them told me that he liked me because I think so differently than he does.… They like to talk about how they feel, and sometimes play word games. Witty comments also seem to
be appreciated by men with Mars in Gemini, since they invariably catch the meaning immediately and laugh” (AQ).

Another woman made these comments: “… these individuals are very open to sexual experience—curious, open-minded, willing to try anything once, so to speak. They like a communicative rapport with their sexual partners.… The sex drive indications of this sign placement seem to be stronger for women than men: men seem more content to think about or talk about sex, while the women seem more prone to go out and do it” (AQ).

Grant Lewi includes in his Gemini Mars evaluations some particularly acute observations: “You are rarely bored or boring, but you can wear others and yourself out by the ferocity of your attack on life. You are extraordinarily aware of the world around you; sense perceptions are acute, swift, probably accurate, and you are voluble in expressing what you take in. Energy flows naturally to the tongue; you are a better talker than a listener.… With any concentrative force, this position gains value, without it, you scatter your energies.… To have a sound mind, you must also have a sound body. You must beware of neglecting your physical well-being through letting your energies dam up in the pools of the mind” (AFM).

A perceptive interviewee mentioned the following about both men and women with this Mars placement: “For Mars in Gemini people, talk
is
 doing something! They are good at connecting people and often play at matchmaking. They can be gossip mongers. Never tell them anything you want kept confidential” (AI).

Mars in Libra

When the planet of aggression and self-assertion is located in the sign of relationship and cooperation, there is inevitably a dynamic tension within the person. Usually there is some difficulty in knowing
exactly what one wants and in expressing a consistent aim or attitude. It is therefore difficult to achieve one’s desires, because the
modus operandi
 is indecisive—allowing frustration and anger to build up. For example, a delightful charm may suddenly shift to a disarmingly blunt directness of expression. These folks’ physical energy and state of mind are strongly affected by whom they’re with and also by aesthetic influences, and their continual search for fairness causes endless deliberation as well. Let’s examine a few of their many conflicting tendencies. Mars wants confrontation whereas Libra (a Venus sign) seeks cooperation. They enjoy (Venus) argument and debate (Mars), especially because an unusual sharpness of mind usually accompanies this placement and is often expressed as an intellectual competitiveness or as a long-term professional or artistic ambition. These folks can be persuasive or argumentative, depending on which way the Libra scales are tipping at the moment. They are really quite unpredictable people, with a tendency to vacillate constantly. They weigh everything in their mind, including how they imagine others will react to possible actions. The rough planet Mars in the refined, kindly sign of Libra can make for many other striking contrasts: ardent affections one minute juxtaposed with cold aloofness the next; or a notable artistic sensitivity in one situation contrasted with vulgar tastes or expressions in another. So many inherent conflicts arise in the Libra Mars personality because there is a constant search for perfect balance in life, in self-expression, and in love and sex. But the resulting ambiguity and indecisiveness can drive this person and others nuts. Mars here forcefully
energizes
 the sign known for
partnership
, but as one questionnaire concisely put it, “Mars in Libra is exasperating. By the time they decide whether they want you or not, you’re not interested” (AQ). The ambiguity and unpredictability of this placement are summed up concisely by a woman with Libra Mars who wrote: “Mars in Libra produces a great need to
understand
 in order to have a basis from which to act—leading to endless analysis, followed often
by impulsive action that is completely in contradiction with the results of the analysis” (AQ).

HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 L
IBRA

I have found the following formulations to express most accurately the core psychological qualities of Mars in Libra:

Asserts self sociably, cooperatively, charmingly, with a direct, energetic interest in one-to-one relationships.

Initiative and drive are tactfully and tactically directed toward balance, fairness, and harmony.

Physical energy and decisiveness are strongly affected by one’s closest relationships and by aesthetic influences, and may be hindered by the desire to weigh all the options.

As already mentioned, these people are always seeking the perfect balance, and this applies to their image of the ideal relationship as well. For both men and women, one drawback with this planetary position is that they are always
comparing
 people (consciously or subconsciously) as they act upon their usually strong attraction to the opposite sex. This makes it difficult to live in the present and to appreciate the particular person they are currently involved with who may have real affection for them. Both sexes are very
interested
 in romance and relationships, and they sometimes even make quite a detailed study of it. Also, both men and women with Libra Mars are quite
dependent
 on close and regular interaction with another person—be it one partner or a number of
close
 friendships—as a source of energy and direction in life. Both tend to have quite a refined attitude toward sex, and they want it to be very personal—sometimes even a bit formal or polite—but certainly considerate and not tainted with abusiveness or excessive “animalistic behavior.” Although those with Mars in Libra are often so “nice” as to be overly apologetic, my
interviews and questionnaires reveal that these folks are often seen by others to be cold as ice once the other person in the relationship wants more than a pleasant demeanor. Like Mars in
all
 the Air signs, mental images
create
 the emotional and sexual excitement, but they are often rather like
reflections
 of real, deep-seated emotions than powerful, immediate experience.

Women with Mars in Libra vacillate between aggressive action and passivity, between the desire for energetic sex and soft romance. Powerful, willful Mars in a sign associated with affectionate, artistic Venus produces some unpredictable personality and interpersonal fireworks as well as some remarkable creativity, both verbal and artistic. These women have a constant need for close relationship and for the energy and stimulation that it provides, but their need for self-validation through getting attention or affection seems to prompt them to initiate many relationships for the early excitement rather than to work hard to sustain a partnership through self-discipline and practical duty. If they can make a commitment—and keep their fantasies down-to-earth and their life habits grounded with a long-term perspective—they can benefit from
much steadier energy
 and a clearer sense of what they really want to achieve in life. They are particularly attracted to men who are intelligent, cultured, sophisticated, and—preferably—very good-looking. If he is not verbally skilled, he’d better be gorgeous or artistically attuned. Fairness is a god to her, and she insists on a fifty-fifty relationship; part of the deal is that she has her own independent space and social and intellectual activity. For all their interest in and need of close relationship, these women do not want it too intense, and that applies to the sexual dimension also. As one interviewee said, “I just don’t have much stamina” (AI). (The overall energy expression will, however, depend on other chart factors as well.) These women are a bit particular aesthetically, and this strongly influences their preferences in sexual activity. One Libra Mars woman emphasized in an interview that she had learned sex procedures
through poetry and romantic novels. Women with this placement are very
personal
, and—as demonstrated in many interviews and questionnaires—also extremely
visual
. Most want to keep some light on during love-making so they can focus on the
person
 they are with, not just the body or emotions as many others might. They like to bask in the personal pleasure being given.

Men with Mars in Libra balance their expression of masculinity and the male ego with a surprising subtlety and an awareness of the other person’s needs and reactions. More than one respondent to questionnaires emphasized that men with this position are unusually considerate of women not only romantically and sexually, but in all areas of life. They like the company of women and are inspired by beauty. Their clear preference for peaceful, smoothly pleasant relationships makes them dislike quarrels or emotional hysteria, for they want to keep everything on the rational plane. But in some cases, their desire to please their partner leads to their being dominated in various ways. They often feel comfortable letting the woman take charge. They
desperately
 need stimulating and romantic interaction to energize their physical and creative life, and they want a very personal approach to sexual expression.

Note the insights into Libra Mars in the following quotes from interviews, questionnaire replies, and other writings:

One man with Mars in Libra wrote, “A lot of my energy goes into ideas, and thinking about projects that I want to start. And of course, thinking about relationships. A lot of my time I’m throwing ideas around in my head, writing articles and speeches in my mind. And I put a lot of energy into personal relationships” (AQ).

A large number of people, in both questionnaires and interviews, touched on a common theme for Libra Mars that is rarely described in astrology literature. It is difficult even to summarize this consensus, but it includes a gentle lightness of touch, and an alternating,
give-and-take, closeness-and-distance theme that is remarkable. Listen to the following quotes taken from many different people:

One woman said, “Sex is adult play, but it must be relaxing” (AI).

A man remarked of a Libra Mars woman how she was able to provide closeness when distance developed between them: “She was often actively peacemaking—often through sex—when there was friction in the relationship. She took the initiative to solve problems during stressful or irritated times” (AI).

As one woman stated about Libra Mars men, they have a light, playful attitude, giving lots of space to the woman (which would be perceived as
too much
 space by watery or earthy women), which airy women feel to be conducive to their free self-expression. She described this kind of man’s approach as “touch and release, touch and release alternating—a loose playful vibration, not at all intense or possessive” (AI).

One man, commenting on a Libra Mars woman, also emphasized the
subtle
 reactions of this placement: “Not powerfully, outwardly orgasmic, but gently orgasmic—a series of openings to different levels” (AI).

Comments from an interview with a particularly outspoken Libra Mars woman expand the portrait further. She did an informal study comparing men of different nationalities and cultures and their styles in bed, and she also remarked, “Giving away sex easily sure made me popular.” She spent much of college arguing, and rather tactlessly, “even unnecessarily stirring up conflict when everything was going well.” Finding conflict stimulating (Mars [conflict] in Libra [personal interaction]), she said, “I hate people who always want to smooth things out with others” (AI).

Grant Lewi adds some accurate assessments: “You must strive for true cooperativeness of spirit, instead of being alternately as meek as a lamb and as mad as a hornet.… You require moderation in the social and love instincts which tend to run away with you, upset your life and destroy your peace” (AFM).

“For both males and females, Mars in Libra is great at promoting their
partner
, sometimes though to the denial of their own needs. Not self-starters or very original, but Libra Mars is a good person to have on your
team”
 (AI).

Mars in Aquarius

When the planet of individual self-assertion and personal independence is found in the sign of uncompromising freedom and rebellion, the combination results in a particularly unpredictable expression of energy—sometimes manifesting as a remarkably innovative originality and other times as a
contrariness
 bordering on the eccentric or sheer crankiness. Extremism characterizes some of their ideas, and they have a fondness—especially in youth—for revolutionary solutions that promise easy reform of societies, organizations, or fields of study. At their best, they powerfully express a great objectivity and a passion for knowledge with a
truly scientific
, experimental attitude based on actual experience and trial and error rather than on conservative, materialistic dogma. These folks are extremely strong willed, although this is often not evident at first contact since they are usually quite attuned to social expectations. Thus, they try to get along with everyone, but they can also be domineering, especially about what they think they
know
. Still, their certainty about what they know can have advantages in many fields. For example, some very effective, patient teachers have this placement, and such work provides a good channel for this energy. Another common professional focus at which
they excel is championing humanitarian causes, scientific advances, or social activism. They tend to be very energized by groups and thus are commonly involved in working with organizations, quite often in a position of leadership. In fact, planning for the future or having a sense of mission about group or intellectual goals is often these people’s real passion.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 A
QUARIUS

Because a number of my good friends have this placement, I have been particularly alert over many years to the core psychological qualities expressed by Mars in Aquarius:

Asserts self intelligently, individualistically, eccentrically, and independently.

Achievement of goals can be thwarted by rebelliousness, but reforming, revolutionizing urge can be channeled into creative innovations.

Physical energy and sex drive are stimulated by a sense of freedom, experimentation, and the excitement of new possibilities and new ideas; detachment and scientific objectivity can hinder the expression of emotion and passionate desires.

Given their innate loyalty, but also their rebellious craving for freedom and experimentation without restriction, both men and women with Mars in Aquarius can paradoxically be characterized as “faithful but not rigid.” Aquarius is one of the most loyal signs, and individuals with this placement often stay committed for decades not only to important people in their lives, but also to certain ideas, causes, ideals, or principles. And this loyalty, together with an acute consciousness of their principles and beliefs, has a direct impact on their attitudes toward close relationships and potential partnerships. Another paradox that affects their approach to relationships is that,
although believing in a great deal of freedom as a matter of principle, they also love being
right
 and
being in control
. Often very observant in romantic and sexual encounters, they nevertheless remain aloofly detached as they coolly consider their own experiences. Hence, they are often awkward when it comes to actual participation in sex or other forms of physical contact. They appear to be stuck in their heads and have trouble relaxing their high-strung minds enough to sink into their bodies and feelings. It seems as if they sense a huge gap between their mental approach to intimate experience and the demands of actual physical and emotional interchange, with its resulting entanglements. This makes them even more cautious about becoming involved.

Women with Mars in Aquarius are—to say the least—nontraditional. They want nothing to do with the traditional feminine social roles; in fact, they energetically rebel against them. The first female leather-jacket-wearing motorcyclist I ever met (thirty or forty years before it became more common) had Mars in this sign. This woman was also arrested once for nude sunbathing on her front lawn. (Of course, since her Sun was also in Aquarius, she was an extremely assertive example of this placement!) Unpredictability is the only thing predictable about these women. And thus, predictably, their approach to sex is unconventional, experimental, and rebellious, but also totally guided by their current concepts and theories. Hence, although open-minded, by nature many of these women are so mentally detached as to be somewhat asexual. (Much depends on the other chart factors.) Not really known for cultivating eroticism or an easy expression of affection or passion through physical and emotional channels, these women are perhaps the most sexually impatient of all Mars signs (although they would have strong competition from Aries Mars, who always wants to be first!). These women are naturally attracted to others who acknowledge their need for freedom and who also express a commitment to it for themselves, as well as to a stimulating,
socially conscious, original kind of intellectual life and lifestyle. Another feature brought out in a few interviews and questionnaires is that even the sexual expression of this Mars placement is erratic and unpredictable. Occasionally, these detached, seemingly cold people will suddenly surprise and even shock the other person by coming on very aggressively and impulsively, as if their instincts have finally broken through their mental barriers.

(Note: Many observations in the last paragraph can also be applied to men with Aquarius Mars, because there is less contrast between the modes of expression in men and women of this Mars sign than any other Mars sign. Perhaps this is because, of all twelve zodiac signs, it is the Mars attunement
least related
 to the physical dimension of life.)

Men with Aquarius Mars surprise others with their unpredictable and often unique insights and ideas, which they sometimes express with sharp humor. They themselves cannot control when such originality will surface, but they and others are intellectually stimulated by it. In a romantic or potential partnership scenario, it is important for them to demonstrate their male ego by showing how clever and intellectually and socially sophisticated they are. They seem to love the verbal banter and flirtation of early romance, sometimes displaying little interest in physical sex and acting quite passive emotionally. One female interviewee remarked on the seeming erotic innocence of Mars in Aquarius men and described them as having a “cutesy, little-boy quality” (AI). Others described them as moody and erratic, and as frustratingly unpredictable in relationships. There is a significant pride in this type of man; but problems arise when he insists on being treated not only with consideration but also in some cases with
excessive
 respect, which obviously then distances him from anyone with whom he might want to have closer contact.

Some particularly perceptive insights into Mars in Aquarius are evident in the following quotes from interviews, questionnaire responses, and other sources:

A very insightful woman wrote: “Mars in Aquarius women are intellectually inclined; they like to conceptualize and philosophize and argue. They need a lot of mental stimulation from other people and are attracted to men who buck the social mainstream. But they tend to be a little domineering—fixed in their ways and not real flexible; restless and not easily pinned to any kind of commitment. Their energy is also not real consistent: sometimes great bursts of energy and ambition and at other times no enthusiasm or energy. It’s either on or off. They’re not extremely personal in their attention to others; it’s hard for them to focus in on individuals since they always have a collective inner vision that superimposes itself on people, so that people become ‘types’ instead of unique entities” (AQ).

A thirty-eight-year-old man commented in an interview on the two Aquarius Mars women he had (short) relationships with: “They were both really interesting in that they had lots of varied and unusual interests and got truly excited about new ideas and self-development. But in bed both were not only extremely impatient, but also unbelievably insensitive. I am a person who likes to soak in the other person’s energies and to fully experience any intimate, sharing encounter deeply and I prefer not to rush it. One woman, right in the middle of love-making, blurted out, ‘Aren’t you done yet?!’ The other one, the next morning, rebuffed my interest in more sex with the off-the-cuff remark, ‘Forget it. You take forever!’ without so much as a hug or a smirk” (AI).

Debbi Kempton Smith doesn’t hold back her views on this Mars sign: “High on technique—when you
bother
—but you are mucho low on understanding. Passion is
nice
 (your favorite word), but it scares the living cucarachas out of you to fall in love. You long to fall, desperately, yet beyond a certain point intimacy terrifies you.… This keeps you nice and safe, and damned lonesome. You’re bright and you need an intelligent mate, but with your bizarre take-it-or-leave-it attitude, only idiots bother with you. There is an
answer. Stop looking. Turn to some Good Cause. When you glow with zeal for making the world a better place and mean it, you’re the biggest turn-on of the lot. Then someone worthy of your lofty goals will find you.… You’re a cool rebel, you think, and you’re hostile to doing the things people expect of you in polite society. You think that stuff’s corny. What you don’t realize is that you hurt people and act like a boor” (SSN).

The following perceptive comments were submitted in a questionnaire reply by a Mars in Aquarius woman who speaks specifically in terms of
energy
. I think virtually everything she says applies to both sexes with Aquarius Mars, and the type of energy flow she describes can also be applied to Aquarius Sun and Aquarius Moon people: “My energy is extremely sporadic, alternating between bursts of activity and periods of lethargy. I like to conceptualize and think about things more than I actually like to do them. I’ve always felt that if this kind of energy flow were related to a metaphor for the process of breathing [Note the Air element reference!—SA], my particular flow would be gasps, not steady inhalation and exhalation. I always seem to be standing outside of myself thinking about what I’m doing.… Sexually speaking I function in much the same gasp-like manner; either I am fixedly pursuing satisfaction, or I am not interested at all, and either way my mind is much more engaged than my body” (AQ).

Grant Lewi’s observations provide a different perspective on this sometimes-baffling Mars placement: “… you are a pretty high-strung person. To translate your energies away from nerves and into channels of material and social progress is to achieve the best that this position has to offer.… you may become set in your ways and desires and … if you run into difficulties with the world … you will tend to feel sorry for yourself, and perhaps to console yourself in ways that block your progress” (AFM).

Person-to-Person Astrology: Energy Factors in Love, Sex and Compatibility

16

Mars in the Water Signs

Mars in any of the Water signs combines energy with emotional intensity; thus, people with this placement react strongly to all life experiences, and they demonstrate emotionally charged types of commitments, ambitions, and desires. These individuals are driven toward certain goals or desires by rather unconscious, often compulsive forces; and they frequently assert themselves in quite
indirect
 ways that many other people have trouble deciphering. These folks prefer a subtle, intuitive, rather quiet
modus operandi
 to achieve their aims. And they are often only partially aware of their own ambitions and goals, until they test how it
feels
 by experimenting. In short, deeper
yearnings
—for both security and life’s meaning—motivate much of the behavior and actions of those with Mars in the Water element. And in fact, these deeper motivations are more real to them than any concept or practical consideration. (The reader should also consult
Chapter 8
 to review my previous comments on Mars in this element.)

Other keys to understanding Mars in the Water signs are as follows:

Getting things done through intuitive methods of operation comes naturally to these folks. Their ultimate aims and desires are often hidden from others and are sometimes even a mystery to themselves. But these people must protect their sensitive reactions; in fact, they are usually
overly sensitive
 when viewed from the perspective of the gross outer world. Their veiled but persistent intentions may frustrate more blunt, matter-of-fact people. However, their apparently
circuitous approach can often yield surprisingly creative results. As one questionnaire respondent who has Mars in Cancer said, “People don’t realize how much I get done and how much I assert my will simply because I don’t let it show; I don’t come out screaming and commanding. I move along more subtle channels” (AQ).

For those with Mars in Water, the physical body is merely a conduit for expressing concentrated emotional energy, and sexual sharing invariably engages especially powerful emotions and sensitivities. The physical aggressive energy increases in parallel with how emotionally intimate and safe the person feels with the partner.

The intuitive qualities found in those with a Water sign Mars confer unique problem-solving abilities that can be useful in all sorts of professions and fields of endeavor. These folks are also particularly gifted in dealing with other people in any situations that require active empathy and caring. The healing arts and helping professions are therefore areas in which they excel, although their intuitive sharpness can also help them excel in business, research, teaching, and strategic planning.

Mars in Cancer

Those with Mars in the hypersensitive, moody sign of Cancer may have trouble demonstrating what they really feel or truly desire. However, when they succeed in expressing such sensitivity and revealing such personal feelings, their partner may feel close to them in an especially intimate way. Virtually all of this chapter’s introductory commentary can be directly applied to understanding Mars in Cancer’s
modus operandi
 in all areas of life. (You might even want to re-read the starting paragraphs of this chapter!) Those with this placement are so often unclear about and unconscious of
what they really want
 that other people may feel they are intentionally avoiding
issues and evading acknowledging their own real desires. Therefore, those dealing with Cancer Mars people often experience considerable frustration trying to figure out what’s going on or to elicit a clear expression of preference or intention from these elusive souls. Mars in Cancer people sometimes appear to simply be hiding, not admitting what they really want, which of course is an effective protection against failure and—in relationships—against feeling rejected. I have yet to figure out how conscious some of these folks really are about their own true desires; I do know that, deep down, this is a tenacious, purposeful sign, so I think they are often playing games and just don’t want to be pinned down. They usually have a rather focused
emotional
 commitment to a goal. There is in fact a childlike innocence about these people, as well as about the games they play in life; and it can be part of their charm, so long as you do not require a definite, concrete response from them.

T
HE
 E
SSENCE OF
 M
ARS IN
 C
ANCER

I have distilled the following core psychological qualities for Cancer Mars after many years of observing not only clients, but also many good friends with this planetary position:

Asserts self sensitively, shyly, indirectly, intuitively, and sympathetically.

Initiative and willpower can be hindered by moodiness and cautious self-protection, but capable of fearless action to support loved ones; preserves tradition and one’s roots.

Physical and sexual energy are stimulated by feeling cared for and protected; unconscious fears and vulnerabilities can inhibit decisive action.

Both men and women with Mars in Cancer tend to have a strong interest in sex that surprises many people since these folks are usually
rather shy and reserved in their public expression. Mars in Cancer combines sexual aggressiveness with passivity and receptivity. There is much emotional give-and-take with the partner, and the more emotional flow and rapport there is, the more sexually aroused the person gets. Generally sympathetic and understanding, these people crave being wanted and needed; and they have the ability to make the partner also feel
needed
 in an intimate way. As one woman said in her questionnaire: “Thoughtful and concerned about the feelings of their partner—they seem to like to get
very close
 to the person with whom they’re having a relationship. Warm personality; they have the ability to make the partner feel wanted” (AQ). Also, both sexes are attracted to someone who has obvious needs for help, support, encouragement, or other kinds of nurturing. This can lead in some cases to getting involved with someone who is particularly needy or manipulative, chronically dependent or depressive. Therefore, it is important that individuals with this Mars placement sort out their feelings and clarify their motivations in any intimate relationship; but that of course is the very thing they find most difficult. Their moods, fears, or concern about emotional vulnerability can diminish their clarity of mind, reduce their initiative, and inhibit their sex drive as well. But even when they are clear about what they want to go after, their approach is very
indirect
, and they rarely say what they are thinking.

Women with Cancer Mars love closeness and intimacy, and sex is a means of feeling that bond. They crave security and emotional empathy. Often expressing a sort of archetypal feminine style and energy, these women can tune in on men very well both emotionally and physically. They sympathize with men, especially those kindly or sensitive types that they are especially attracted to—gentle men who are protective, domestic, reassuring, and even a bit clinging. In fact, one of the contradictions in their nature is that, although they may say they want a strong, assertive, self-sufficient man, deep down they prefer a rather weak or at least passive or particularly needy fellow.
These women need someone they can mother and really prefer to dominate the relationship and the home life in particular. They tend alternately to yield to the man’s will and then to resent it! Further problems develop in the relationship if the man turns out to be independent and self-motivating, and thus impossible to dominate. The woman in that case may then find him very unappealing and complain that he’s insensitive, that he’s never home, and so on. So this position is a paradox on many levels.

Mars in Cancer men are usually quite gentle and capable of being so traditionally romantic that they can even seem to swoon over their lady love. Probably better able to understand most women’s needs than any other Mars sign, men with Mars in Cancer are also the most domestic of all men, often being excellent cooks, and good at gardening and other home-loving projects. They are instinctively protective and often strive hard to be good providers in a traditional way. There is always a strong emphasis on home and family themes (both current and past family groups), but—even though they usually love their kids and are very attached to them—some men with this Mars placement seem quite impatient with their kids or seem to take them so much for granted as to ignore their
real
 personal needs. For women there is an easy way to alienate such a man: act impatient, demanding, and insensitive. When these guys’ feelings are hurt, they instinctively withdraw; and you may not see their real feelings demonstrated again or detect any evidence of erotic interest in you for years if they truly felt rejected.

Particularly perceptive insights into Cancer Mars are evident in the following quotes from interviews, questionnaire replies, and other writings:

Debbi Kempton Smith points out how
kind
 these people are (to virtually everyone), and thus they are often taken advantage of—sometimes because of their naïve innocence or because someone
played on their sympathies. As she writes, “You are acutely sensitive to your mate’s emotional needs because you are genuinely kind, so you are better at keeping a mate than any other Mars position. Relax. Give yourself credit for sneaking up on people with your suave, subtle, sweet understanding—the good old Sideways School of Seduction” (SSN).

In
The Astrologer’s Handbook
, Julia Parker nicely expresses some of Cancer Mars’ finer attributes: “Here, Mars picks up the tenacity of Cancer so that a terrific effort is made to achieve whatever is important to the individual.… This is a marvelously sensuous placing for Mars, and the expression and enjoyment of sex is usually extremely colourful; also, a sensitive and intuitive quality gives an instinctive realization of the partner’s needs” (AHB).

There is a theme of
containment
 that comes through in a number of interviews and questionnaires, which is interesting because Cancer traditionally has been associated with the womb. All Cancer Mars people of both sexes seem to love cuddling, clinging together somewhere safe and warm, and in fact they can sometimes hang on to the loved one so interminably that the partner feels thoroughly smothered! One man, whose only planet in the sign Cancer was Mars, revealed the following very personal disclosure in an interview, probably only because I knew him well and had his trust: “Envelopment in the bedclothes feels like a kind of womb and
allows
 sexual arousal. I need to feel protected and enwrapped in the other person and mutually nourished by her. I want to feel that there is a warm place to crawl into” (AI). Another man, who himself had no planets in Cancer, stated, “I’ve known a number of Mars in Cancer women, and they all have fast sexual response and like having a man
in
 them; they like
containment
 of the partner, not just play or performance” (AI).

A woman with Mars in Cancer commented in her questionnaire: “Sometimes I think that my Mars in a Water sign indicates the ebbing and flowing of my need for sex, like the tides. Sometimes I’m very sexually oriented, and then many days will pass when I just don’t give it all that much thought. I think for me, too, that there is a lot of emotional stuff tied to sex, and Mars in the Water element indicates this” (AQ).

Grant Lewi’s writings confirm a pattern that appeared in much of my research: “Women with this position have a curious way of ruling the roost, at the same time that they think they want a strong brave man to shield them from the world. Men with this position frequently are ruled by their women or by the psychological biases rooted in the mother inheritance” (AFM).

Mars in Scorpio

When Mars is located in Scorpio—a sign with which it has been associated as the “ruling planet” since the era of the ancient Greeks—it is particularly forceful in its expression. Dynamic leadership, a strong sense of mission, passionate commitments, intense desires, and an intuitive know-how based on trusting gut feelings are some of the characteristics expressed by people with Mars in Scorpio. It is certainly one of the most powerful placements for Mars, and this power flows into everything these individuals do, including relationships and sexuality. Whatever they tackle, they do it with persistence, energetic endurance, and a profound intensity of involvement. As Julia Parker writes, “There is a terrific sense of purpose and determination …” (AHB). Their method of operation is intuitive but very deliberate, and it is based on a thorough investigation of all the factors involved and their implications—although most of this “research” is performed by their subconscious mind and communicated to their awareness
through gut feelings. In any case, it takes a lot to make them give up once they have committed to a course of action, and their striking resourcefulness ensures that they will often succeed even at difficult ventures for which they apparently had few advantages or little training. In fact, it is their passionate commitment to a sense of mission, or to confronting a challenge, that makes them effective and a formidable ally or foe. (And there is usually not much in between in their attitudes toward individuals; they are extraordinarily loyal to friends, but never forget a hurt or betrayal.) I always like to emphasize that, although Scorpio has the reputation for a major sexual attunement in their lives, their greatest contribution is to provide
passion
 to anything they are involved in. Lots of signs are quite sexual (Taurus, Capricorn, and Leo, for example), but none of them express the emotional passion that Scorpio does.
